## 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器 [[Back]](#toc)

面向理解与生成统一的连续视觉 token、VAE/ViT、RAE、DINO/SigLIP/MAE latent 与视觉表征重建。

### [1] [ViQ: Text-Aligned Visual Quantized Representations at Any Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27313)
*Xumin Yu, Zuyan Liu, Zhenyu Yang, Yuhao Dong, Shengsheng Qian, Jiwen Lu, Han Hu, Yongming Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器

Importance: 高优先级 (89.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ViQ 提出了一种文本对齐的视觉量化表征框架，通过两阶段训练（文本对齐预训练 + 特征离散化）在离散视觉 token 中平衡语义信息与细节重建，支持任意分辨率输入，在多模态任务上取得与连续高维视觉编码器相当的性能，并显著提升训练效率（20%-70%加速）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散视觉表征难以同时兼顾低层细节重建和高层语义信息：重建导向的表征缺乏语义，而语义强的特征又丢失细节。同时，统一文本与视觉的离散表征对于简化多模态建模和提升训练效率至关重要。

Method: ViQ 将量化学习分为两个阶段：(1) 文本对齐预训练——利用预训练语言模型为视觉编码器提供语义丰富的监督信号，并支持原生分辨率输入；(2) 特征离散化——提出近端表征学习策略逐步压缩特征空间，以及位置感知的逐头量化机制，灵活处理任意分辨率。

Result: 在多模态任务上，ViQ 取得了与使用连续高维视觉特征的 SOTA 多模态视觉编码器相当的竞争性能，同时保持高精度的低层重建。使用 ViQ 进行多模态训练可将效率提升 20%-70%。

Conclusion: ViQ 成功实现了兼顾语义与细节的离散视觉量化表征，支持任意分辨率输入，为统一文本与视觉的离散表征提供了有效方案，并在效率和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ViQ 框架，核心贡献是设计了一种文本对齐的离散视觉量化表征，支持任意分辨率输入，属于连续视觉 tokenizer 与表征自编码器方向。该方向是 [CANONICAL] 条目，完美匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances the user's top-priority continuous language/multimodal model direction with a novel text-aligned discrete visual tokenizer that balances semantics and detail. Signals: continuous visual token, text-aligned quantization, arbitrary resolution, training efficiency 20-70% acceleration, two-stage training

Abstract: A unified representation for text and vision is a natural pursuit, as it enables simpler multimodal modeling and more efficient training. However, representing images as discrete signals in the same way as text inevitably introduces severe information loss. Existing work struggles to balance low-level details and high-level semantics in discrete representations: reconstruction-oriented representations often lack semantic information, whereas semantically stronger features typically suffer from severe loss of detail. We present ViQ, a Visual Quantized Representations framework, which is designed to balance semantics and details in discrete representations while supporting inputs at native resolutions, thereby enabling it to serve as a unified and general discrete representation for arbitrary visual inputs. Our approach structures quantization learning into two stages: text-aligned pre-training and feature discretization. With text-aligned pre-training, we enhance the visual encoder semantic-rich supervision from the pretrained language model and enable it to process native-resolution visual inputs. During discretization, we propose a proximal representation learning strategy to progressively compact the feature space, along with a position-aware head-wise quantization mechanism that enables flexible processing of arbitrary resolutions. Extensive experiments on multimodal tasks demonstrate that ViQ achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art multimodal vision encoders with continuous and high-dimensional visual features, while maintaining high precision in low-level reconstruction. We also show that multimodal training with visual quantized representations largely improves efficiency, yielding up to 20\%-70\% acceleration with different base LLMs and training recipes.

</details>

### [2] [Nemotron-TwoTower: Diffusion Language Modeling with Pretrained Autoregressive Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26493)
*Fitsum Reda, John Kamalu, Roger Waleffe, Mostofa Patwary, Mohammad Shoeybi, Bryan Catanzaro*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (96.67) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: Nemotron-TwoTower 提出了一种双塔扩散语言模型架构，将上下文表示与迭代去噪解耦为两个独立模块：冻结的自回归上下文塔和可训练的扩散去噪塔。在 30B MoE 模型上训练后，保留了 AR 基线 98.7% 的质量，同时实现了 2.42 倍的生成吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型使用单一网络同时承担上下文表示和迭代去噪两个角色，限制了模型在任一角色上的能力。作者希望解耦这两个功能，让每个塔专注于其专长。

Method: 提出 TwoTower 架构：一个冻结的 AR 上下文塔（causal 处理干净 token），一个可训练的扩散去噪塔（双向块注意力，通过交叉注意力关注上下文）。基于 Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B（30B 混合 Mamba-Transformer MoE 模型）实现，采用块级自回归扩散（block-wise autoregressive diffusion）策略。

Result: 在约 2.1T token 上训练后，Nemotron-TwoTower 保留了 AR 基线 98.7% 的质量，同时实现了 2.42 倍的端到端生成吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 解耦上下文表示与去噪的双塔设计是扩散语言模型的有效范式，能够在保持生成质量的同时显著提升推理效率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TwoTower 扩散语言模型，属于连续潜空间中的语言建模方法，与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model and diffusion language modeling, a top priority subtopic, with strong empirical results and open-source release. Signals: continuous language model, diffusion language model, autoregressive context, 30B MoE, 2.42x throughput

Abstract: Diffusion language models offer a promising alternative to autoregressive models due to their potential for parallel and iterative generation. However, existing approaches use a single network for both context representation and iterative denoising, forcing one model to serve both roles and limiting its capacity for either role. We propose TwoTower, a block-wise autoregressive diffusion model that decouples these roles into two towers: a frozen AR context tower that causally processes clean tokens, and a trainable diffusion denoiser tower with bidirectional block attention that refines noisy blocks via cross-attention to the context. Built on Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B, an open-weight 30B hybrid Mamba-Transformer MoE model, and trained on approximately 2.1T tokens, Nemotron-TwoTower retains 98.7% of the autoregressive baseline's quality while offering 2.42X higher wall-clock generation throughput. We release the code and model weights at .

</details>

## 具身世界模型与规划控制 [[Back]](#toc)

### [3] [RMTL: Reinforced Micro-task Learning for Long-Horizon Manipulation with VLM Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26175)
*Anıl Can Ateş, Orhan Kahraman, Cihan Topal*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出RMTL（强化微任务学习），将长时域机器人操作任务分解为多个语言描述的微任务，每个微任务使用独立的VLM提示计算奖励，结合反向课程学习和层次化策略管理，解决了单提示VLM奖励在长时域任务中信号稀疏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的零样本奖励模型使用单一全局提示，在长时域操作任务中奖励信号近乎平坦，智能体难以检测早期进展。手动设计密集奖励函数难以调优且脆弱，从人类演示中学习奖励成本高昂。

Method: 将操作任务分解为少量语言描述的微任务，每个微任务有独立的VLM提示；智能体学习在微任务间切换；使用多视角VLM奖励（当前活跃微任务的提示）并平均多视角以减少遮挡影响；采用反向课程学习逐步增加初始条件难度；先用固定距离规则训练PPO worker，再替换为学习的层次化管理器。

Result: 在Fetch操作环境中，RMTL相比单提示VLM奖励提供了更丰富的奖励信号，使智能体学习更快，验证了微任务分解策略对语言引导强化学习可扩展性的提升。

Conclusion: 将VLM奖励分解为微任务特定的语言提示可以显著提升语言引导强化学习在机器人操作中的可扩展性，层次化策略学习进一步提升了任务完成效果。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人长时域操作任务中的强化学习，使用VLM作为奖励模型，并引入层次化微任务分解与策略学习。这属于具身智能中的机器人操作与规划控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation with VLM rewards, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: VLM reward decomposition, long-horizon manipulation, hierarchical RL, reverse curriculum, Fetch environment

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic manipulation often requires manually designing a dense reward function, which is difficult to tune and often fragile, or learning a reward from human demonstrations or preferences, which can be expensive. A recent line of work uses pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) as zero-shot reward models, replacing these costs with a single text prompt. However, we argue that a single global prompt is too coarse for long-horizon manipulation tasks with randomized initial conditions. The single-prompt VLM reward is near-flat for much of the trajectory, making early progress hard for the agent to detect. We propose Reinforced Micro-Task Learning (RMTL), an approach that decomposes a manipulation task into a small set of language-described micro-tasks and trains the agent to switch between them. At each step, the agent receives a multi-view VLM reward computed using the prompt of the currently active micro-task and averaged across multiple camera views to reduce the effect of view-specific occlusions. A reverse curriculum gradually exposes the agent to harder initial conditions, while a PPO worker is first trained with a fixed distance-based rule that selects the active micro-task. We then replace this rule with a learned hierarchical manager, turning rule-based phase selection into a fully learned hierarchical policy. We instantiate RMTL on the Fetch manipulation environment using three short stage-specific prompts and without additional prompt tuning. Experiments show that RMTL provides more informative reward signals than single-prompt VLM rewards, enabling faster learning. These results suggest that decomposing VLM rewards into micro-task-specific language prompts can substantially improve the scalability of language-guided reinforcement learning for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [4] [TaskNPoint: How to Teach Your Humanoid to Hit a Backhand in Minutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26215)
*Blake Werner, Ilona Demler, Pietro Perona, Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出TaskNPoint训练协议，通过人类教练提供少量示范（每种技能一个演示）和交互窗口定义，结合物理仿真环境中的随机目标采样训练，使人形机器人在单GPU上不到一小时内学会正手击球、反手击球、踢球和抓取放置等动态技能，且零样本泛化到未见目标位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态技能（如网球击球）的关键在于轨迹中极短的一段交互窗口（约20cm的球拍行程），而非大量数据。作者认为人形机器人学习也应像人类一样由教练指导、少量练习，而非依赖海量视频数据。

Method: TaskNPoint协议将教练-学习者分工明确化：人类教练提供离散技能集、每个技能一次演示、交互窗口定义和目标；物理仿真环境通过随机目标采样训练，使单次演示零样本泛化到新目标位置。无需每任务奖励调参。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上成功学会正手/反手击球、踢球和抓取放置，从短人类视频演示出发，单GPU训练不到一小时即可完成，且零样本泛化到未见目标位置。

Conclusion: 动态技能学习可以简化为掌握少量离散动作并练习交互窗口，TaskNPoint协议证明了少量人类示范+物理仿真训练的有效性，为人形机器人快速学习动态技能提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人形机器人学习动态技能（如击球、踢球、抓取），使用物理仿真环境进行训练，属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics skill learning paper but outside user's core continuous multimodal and video generation focus. Signals: humanoid dynamic skill learning, few-shot demonstration, zero-shot generalization, efficient training, world model application

Abstract: How do we learn to hit a tennis backhand? Not from a thousand hours of tennis tournaments on TV - we work with a coach and practice. We argue this is also the right recipe for teaching dynamic skills to humanoid robots. This follows from a structural property of dynamic skills: the outcome is decided by a short, crucial portion of the trajectory - for a backhand, the ~20cm of racket travel around ball contact. Getting this interaction window right requires coordinating the whole motion, so that control, physics, and morphology act in concert. Learning thus reduces to mastering a handful of distinct actions and, for each, practicing until the window comes out right. To this end, we introduce TaskNPoint, a training protocol which makes the coach-learner division of labor explicit. The human coach contributes four inputs: a discrete set of skills (e.g. different shots), one demonstration per skill, identification of the interaction window, and the goal. Learning in a physically realistic simulation environment fills in each action trajectory and provides robustness to unmodeled events. Crucially, randomized target sampling during training lets a single demonstration generalize zero-shot to unseen goal locations. We test this approach on a Unitree G1 humanoid that hits forehands and backhands against balls thrown by a human, kicks incoming soccer balls, and picks and places boxes from novel locations. We find that learning is successful from short human video demonstrations and under an hour of training on a single GPU, with no per-task reward tuning.

</details>


### [5] [NavIsaacLab: Generating Realistic Crowd via Parallel Robot Learning for Benchmarking Human-aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26265)
*Bingyi Xia, Han Bao, Jingyu Zhu, Hanjing Ye, Yuhan Pang, Guangcheng Chen, Liang Lin, Wenjun Xu, Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: NavIsaacLab是一个基于Isaac Lab的机器人导航仿真框架，利用GPU并行物理仿真、轨迹扩散模型和对抗运动学习控制器生成逼真的人群场景，为人类感知导航策略提供训练与评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人感知导航研究缺乏多样化、高质量的仿真场景数据。现有平台多依赖手工规则模拟行人行为，且无法提供丰富的传感器信号（通常假设完美观测），限制了导航策略的训练与评估。

Method: 基于Isaac Lab构建框架，支持照片级真实感场景渲染和GPU并行仿真，为机器人提供实时准确的3D视觉反馈。采用数据驱动方法：轨迹扩散模型生成行人路径，对抗运动学习控制器实现可控的物理仿真行人运动。集成跨尺度多样化场景作为基准。

Result: 在多种导航场景下对现有最先进的人感知导航方法进行了基准测试，验证了框架在生成逼真人群和评估导航策略方面的有效性。

Conclusion: NavIsaacLab提供了一个物理真实、照片级逼真且支持并行仿真的综合框架，有效缓解了人感知导航研究中场景数据稀缺的问题，为训练和评估鲁棒的导航策略奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于Isaac Lab的机器人导航仿真框架，通过物理仿真和轨迹扩散模型生成逼真人群，用于训练和评测人感知导航策略。这属于面向机器人具身智能的仿真环境与规划控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot navigation simulation, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, robot navigation, crowd simulation, Isaac Lab, GPU parallel simulation

Abstract: Robot autonomous navigation that accounts for surrounding human activities is crucial for ensuring both safety and natural human-robot interaction in real-world environments shared by humans and robots. Simulation of complex and diverse navigation scenarios serves as the foundation for training reliable robot navigation policies and accurately evaluating the performance of algorithms, offering an efficient alternative to manual supervision of real data. However, current human-aware navigation research faces significant challenges due to the scarcity of diverse, high-quality scene data. Existing simulation platforms often rely on handcrafted rules to approximate pedestrian behavior and lack the capability to provide extensive sensor signals, typically assuming perfect observations. To address these limitations, this paper presents NavIsaacLab, a comprehensive framework for benchmarking and training human-aware navigation policies through physics-based and photo-realistic simulations of pedestrians and scenes. Based on Isaac Lab, the proposed framework employs photo-realistic scene rendering capabilities and supports parallel simulation on GPU, delivering real-time and accurate 3D visual feedback to robots. To enhance the realism of human behavior, a data-driven approach is employed that incorporates a trajectory diffusion model and an adversarial motion learning controller, enabling controllable, physics-based pedestrian simulation. Furthermore, the integration of diverse cross-scale scenes provides a robust benchmark for state-of-the-art human-aware navigation methods.

</details>


### [6] [Play2Perfect: What Matters in Dexterous Play Pretraining for Precise Assembly?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26428)
*Tyler Ga Wei Lum, Kushal Kedia, C. Karen Liu, Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (57.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Play2Perfect 框架，通过任务无关的灵巧操作预训练（玩耍）获取可复用的操作先验，再微调用于高精度装配任务，在 0.5mm 间隙的紧装配上达到 60% 成功率，样本效率比从零训练高 33 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手在精密装配等接触密集、稀疏奖励的任务中面临数据收集困难和直接强化学习难以探索的问题。现有工作依赖专用夹爪、工具附件和环境固定装置来简化问题。本文认为机器人应先学会"玩耍"（操作多样物体），再精化到精密装配任务。

Method: 提出 Play2Perfect 两阶段 RL 框架：1）任务无关的玩耍预训练阶段，在多样物体和目标上学习可复用的操作先验（抓取、手内重定向、位姿到达）；2）微调阶段，将通用先验适配到精密装配任务，聚焦于接触密集的高精度交互探索。系统研究了物体多样性、训练目标、轨迹多样性和目标精度等关键设计选择。

Result: 预训练先验比从零训练的 RL（即使提供密集多阶段奖励）样本效率高 33 倍。零样本 sim-to-real 迁移在 0.5mm 间隙的紧装配上达到 60% 成功率，在长时序多部件装配和拧螺丝任务上超过 50% 成功率。

Conclusion: 任务无关的玩耍预训练能有效获取可复用的灵巧操作先验，显著提升精密装配任务的样本效率和成功率，且能零样本迁移到真实机器人。物体多样性、目标精度等设计选择对预训练效果有重要影响。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Play2Perfect 框架，通过任务无关的"玩耍"预训练获取可复用的灵巧操作先验（抓取、手内重定向、位姿到达），再微调用于高精度装配任务。这属于具身智能中通过预训练获取世界模型/操作先验，再用于规划控制的范式，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics assembly work but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: dexterous manipulation, sample efficiency, sim-to-real transfer, precise assembly, RL pretraining

Abstract: Multi-fingered robots promise the speed and dexterity of human hands, yet challenging problems such as precise assembly have remained out of reach. These tasks are contact-rich, making data collection for imitation learning difficult, and sparse-reward, making direct exploration with reinforcement learning (RL) intractable. Consequently, prior work has made progress by structuring the problem with specialized grippers, tool attachments, and environment fixtures. In this work, we argue that before a robot can perfect precise assembly, it must first learn to play. We further ask the question: what factors in the process of learning to play matter for precise assembly? We propose Play2Perfect, an RL framework for task-agnostic pretraining through play on diverse objects and goals, which is then perfected on precise assembly. The goal of play is to acquire reusable manipulation priors, such as grasping, in-hand reorientation and pose reaching. Finetuning then adapts this general prior to assembly, focusing exploration on the final contact-rich, high-precision interactions needed for success. We systematically study key design choices in play pretraining, including object diversity, training objective, trajectory diversity, and goal precision. We show that our prior is 33x more sample-efficient than RL training from scratch, even when provided with dense, multi-stage rewards. We demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, achieving 60% success on tight insertions with only 0.5 mm contact clearance, and over 50% success on long-horizon multi-part assembly and screwing.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的多智能体控制方法在 sim-to-real 迁移中严重依赖精确的动力学建模或系统辨识，且策略在低维控制空间中学习，对动力学不匹配高度敏感，导致在复杂环境中成本高昂且脆弱。现有方法难以有效应对动力学不匹配带来的迁移性能下降。

Method: 1) 效果对齐（Effect Alignment）：通过闭环控制将随机环境结构与离散语义动作结合，将策略学习提升到语义抽象层面，降低对底层动力学的依赖。2) 动作同步机制（Action Synchronization）：缓解智能体间动作时序不匹配问题，增强系统的时间一致性。

Result: 在四个多智能体导航任务上的实验表明，该方法相比主流迁移方法显著提升了训练效率，并在真实场景中取得了更高的成功率，验证了其在动力学不匹配下的鲁棒性和部署稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过效果对齐将策略抽象到语义层面，结合动作同步机制，可以有效缓解多智能体控制中 sim-to-real 迁移对动力学不匹配的敏感性，提升系统的鲁棒性和实际部署效果。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多智能体控制中的 sim-to-real 迁移问题，通过效果对齐和语义抽象策略提升对动力学不匹配的鲁棒性，属于机器人/具身智能中的策略学习与规划控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on sim-to-real transfer for multi-agent control, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Multi-agent control, Sim-to-real transfer, Effect alignment, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Complex multi-agent control tasks remain challenging for traditional rule-based and model-based approaches, motivating the adoption of learning-based methods. However, learning-based methods often struggle with sim-to-real transfer because they rely on accurate dynamics modeling or system identification and learn policies in low-level control spaces that are highly sensitive to dynamics mismatch, making them costly and fragile in complex environments. To address this issue, we propose a sim-to-real method for multi-agent control, which is insensitive to dynamics mismatch via effect alignment. Our method combines random environmental structure with discrete semantic actions through closed-loop control, elevating policy learning to a semantic abstraction level. Additionally, we develop an action synchronization mechanism that mitigates inter-agent action timing mismatches, thereby enhancing the temporal consistency of the system. Experiments on four multi-agent navigation tasks demonstrate that our method substantially improves training efficiency over mainstream transfer methods and achieves higher success rates in real-world scenarios, thereby improving the robustness and deployment stability of multi-agent systems under dynamics mismatch.

</details>


### [8] [Tactile-WAM: Touch-Aware World Action Model with Tactile Asymmetric Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26663)
*Siyu Wu, Linjing You, Junjie Zhu, Yaozu Liu, Changhao Zhang, Jian Liu, Weiqiang Wang, Qi Li, Jituo Li, Hengshuang Zhao*

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: Tactile-WAM 提出了一种触觉感知的世界动作模型，通过非对称触觉注意力机制（TAAM）解决触觉污染问题，在接触丰富的机器人操作任务中显著提升了动作预测成功率和视频预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的机器人操作任务（如插入、装配、搜索、重定向）中，仅依赖视觉信息的世界动作模型（WAM）无法捕捉滑动、卡滞、接触法线等关键触觉信号，导致物理上不完整的预测。然而，直接将触觉 token 注入视觉模型会导致"触觉污染"——稀疏、局部的触觉信号干扰视觉动力学建模，反而降低预测质量。

Method: 提出 Tactile-WAM，核心是触觉非对称注意力机制（TAAM），包含两个组件：(1) VideoClean 掩码——阻止视频查询（query）访问触觉键/值（key/value）token，同时保留动作查询对触觉 token 的访问，从而保护视觉预测不受触觉污染；(2) 触觉感知偏置（touch-aware bias）——基于预测的触觉变化量调制动作注意力对触觉 token 的关注度。模型在 ManiFeel 数据集上训练，采用扩散去噪框架联合预测视频、动作和触觉状态。

Result: 在 ManiFeel 数据集上，Tactile-WAM 整体平均成功率提升 38.9%，在接触丰富任务上提升 86%。消融实验验证了 TAAM 各组件（VideoClean 掩码和触觉感知偏置）的有效性，同时视频预测质量也得到改善。

Conclusion: Tactile-WAM 通过非对称注意力机制有效解决了触觉模态与视觉模态在世界动作模型中的融合问题，证明了触觉信息对接触丰富机器人操作任务的关键价值，为多模态世界模型的设计提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Tactile-WAM，一种触觉感知的世界动作模型（World Action Model），用于机器人接触丰富的操作任务（如插入、装配）。它属于世界模型方向下的具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）这一规范子主题，因为其核心是面向机器人动作生成与规划的世界模型，且涉及触觉模态与动作条件预测。

Importance reason: Strong robotics world model paper but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: world action model, tactile asymmetric attention, contact-rich manipulation, video prediction, action prediction

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) generate actions together with predicted futures, offering a powerful interface for robot decision making. In contact-rich manipulation, however, visually plausible futures can be physically incomplete: insertion, assembly, search, and reorientation often depend on slip, jamming, contact normals, or small alignment errors that are weakly visible or hidden in RGB. A natural solution is to predict future tactile states, however, we identify tactile pollution, a failure mode where unconstrained tactile-token injection degrades video and action prediction by forcing a visual dynamics model to absorb sparse, local, event-driven contact signals. To address this, we propose Tactile-WAM, a touch-aware WAM with a Tactile Asymmetric Attention Mechanism (TAAM). TAAM combines a VideoClean mask, which blocks video-query access to tactile key/value tokens while preserving action-query access, with a touch-aware bias for action attention. The VideoClean mask protects visual prediction while keeping contact information available for action generation; the touch-aware bias is derived from predicted touch changes and modulates action attention to tactile tokens during denoising. On ManiFeel, Tactile-WAM improves the mean success rate by 38.9% overall and by 86% on contact-rich tasks.

</details>

*Sajid Ansari, Arthi, Girish Varma, Antony Thomas*

TL;DR: 本文提出从原始RGB-D点云中学习7-DOF机械臂在杂乱场景中的运动可行性预测，构建了包含270万抓取可行性标签的大规模基准，并评测了MLP、体素CNN和点云Transformer三类模型，最佳模型GRASPFC-PTX在未见物体上达到0.996 AUROC，显著快于基于采样的运动规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的运动规划器（SBMP）在杂乱环境中进行不可行规划尝试会消耗大量计算资源，而现有的不可行性认证方法局限于低维配置空间且假设简化的几何环境。因此需要直接从原始RGB-D观测中高效预测运动可行性。

Method: 构建了包含88个扫描物体、190个杂乱桌面场景、270万抓取可行性标签的大规模基准数据集。评测了三类代表性分类器：MLP、体素CNN和基于点云的Transformer架构（GRASPFC-PTX），在匹配的训练条件下进行对比。

Result: 最佳模型GRASPFC-PTX（点云Transformer）在未见物体上达到0.996 AUROC，预测速度显著快于SBMP方法。

Conclusion: 从原始RGB-D点云中学习运动可行性预测是可行的，点云Transformer架构在该任务上表现优异，能够高效替代传统SBMP的不可行性检测步骤。

Classification reason: 论文研究7-DOF机械臂在杂乱场景中的运动可行性预测，直接关联机器人运动规划与控制，属于具身世界模型与规划控制这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics motion feasibility from point clouds, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Robotics, motion feasibility, point cloud, cluttered environments, 7-DOF manipulator

Abstract: Motion feasibility prediction plays a central role in robotics, particularly in task and motion planning and manipulation. A major bottleneck for this problem in cluttered environments is that infeasible planning attempts by Sampling-based motion planners (SBMPs) can incur substantial computational cost. Also existing approaches for infeasibility certification are limited to low-dimensional configuration spaces and often assume simplified geometric environments represented by primitive objects with known parameters. We study the complementary problem of learning motion feasibility prediction directly from raw RGB-D observations for a 7-DOF manipulator operating in realistic cluttered scenes. We introduce the first large-scale benchmark for this setting, comprising 2.7M grasp feasibility labels over 88 scanned objects and 190 cluttered tabletop scenes. We benchmark three representative classifier families spanning MLP- based, volumetric-CNN, and point-cloud-based Transformer architectures under matched training conditions. Our best model, GRASPFC-PTX (a point-cloud transformer), achieves an AUROC of 0.996 on Novel objects while providing predictions significantly faster than SBMPs.

</details>

*Yi Lu, Shenghao Ren, Tianyu Xiong, Zhaoxiang Li, Jiaqi Li, He Zhang, Tao Yu, Qiu Shen, Xun Cao*

TL;DR: PressMimic 提出将压力作为统一模态，贯穿人形机器人运动捕捉与控制的全流程。在感知阶段，FRAPPE++ 融合 RGB 和压力估计 3D 姿态与全局运动；在控制阶段，压力监督策略（PSP）将压力信号引入强化学习，实现物理一致的运动模仿。同时构建了 MotionPRO 大规模同步数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人运动模仿主要依赖视觉运动捕捉和运动学模仿，忽略了接触动力学，导致脚滑、穿透地面、不稳定等伪影。作者希望利用压力作为物理接地信号，弥合感知与控制之间的鸿沟，实现物理一致的人形运动模仿。

Method: 提出 PressMimic 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) FRAPPE++，一种融合 RGB 和压力图的多模态模型，联合估计 3D 姿态和全局运动，利用压力提供的接触与支撑约束解决视觉估计的歧义性；(2) 压力监督策略（PSP），将压力导出的信号引入强化学习，在执行过程中实现物理一致的接触模式。同时构建了 MotionPRO 数据集，包含同步的 RGB、压力和运动捕捉数据。

Result: 实验表明，引入压力信号提高了运动估计精度、轨迹一致性和执行稳定性，有效减少了脚滑、地面穿透等伪影，验证了压力作为物理接地信号在连接感知与控制方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 压力是一种有效的物理接地信号，能够桥接感知与控制，实现物理一致的人形机器人运动模仿。PressMimic 展示了将物理传感模态（压力）融入机器人模仿学习全流程的可行性和优势。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人形机器人模仿人类运动，利用压力信号进行运动捕捉与控制，属于具身智能中机器人动作规划与控制的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel pressure modality for humanoid imitation, but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: world model, humanoid robot, pressure sensing, motion capture, reinforcement learning

Abstract: Humanoid motion imitation requires not only accurate perception of human kinematics but also faithful reproduction of physical interactions with the environment. However, existing pipelines rely primarily on vision-based motion capture and kinematic imitation, largely ignoring contact dynamics, leading to artifacts such as foot sliding, floor penetration, and unstable behaviors. In this work, we revisit humanoid motion imitation from the perspective of physical grounding and leverage pressure as a unified modality across perception and control. We present PressMimic, a framework that integrates pressure into the full pipeline from motion capture to humanoid control. In the perception stage, we introduce FRAPPE++, a multimodal model that fuses RGB and pressure to jointly estimate 3D pose and global motion, where pressure provides explicit contact and support constraints to resolve ambiguity in vision-based estimation. In the control stage, we propose a pressure-supervised policy (PSP) that incorporates pressure-derived signals into reinforcement learning, enabling physically consistent contact patterns during execution. We further construct MotionPRO, a large-scale dataset with synchronized RGB, pressure, and motion capture data. Experiments show that pressure improves motion estimation accuracy, trajectory consistency, and execution stability. These results demonstrate that pressure serves as an effective physical grounding signal, bridging perception and control for physically consistent humanoid motion imitation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的机器人操作需要空间接地、任务感知推理和精确控制，但在低数据场景下学习这些能力极具挑战。现有方法在可扩展的任务级推理和显式物理结构之间存在权衡：基于视频的方法在长时序中几何漂移，3D方法通常需要深度传感器，而流/轨迹接口强调运动但缺乏显式的RGB-only几何表征。

Method: SSI-Policy是一个模块化框架，核心是结构化场景接口（SSI）——一种统一的、仅依赖RGB的中间表征，联合编码单目深度特征、语言引导的目标布局和指令条件化的2D运动轨迹。SSI是机器人无关的，可从无动作视频中训练，从而将感知与控制解耦，使下游策略能从少量演示中学习。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，仅用10个演示/任务，SSI-Policy相比最强先前方法提升近15%，且与使用大规模外部预训练的50个演示方法竞争力相当。在13个真实世界任务（涵盖空间推理、跨本体迁移和接触丰富操作）上进一步验证了有效性。消融实验表明几何和运动线索在共享接口中提供互补收益。

Conclusion: SSI-Policy通过结构化场景接口有效解耦感知与控制，在低数据场景下实现了高效的机器人操作学习，验证了RGB-only几何表征与语言引导运动轨迹结合的互补优势，为少样本机器人学习提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SSI-Policy，一种面向机器人操作的模块化框架，通过结构化场景接口（SSI）实现RGB-only的几何表征、语言引导的目标布局和指令条件化的2D运动轨迹，用于少样本机器人操作学习。这属于具身智能中的机器人操作与规划控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation with structured scene interfaces, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Robotic manipulation, RGB-only intermediate representation, Low-data regime, LIBERO benchmark, Decoupled perception and control

Abstract: Real-world robotic manipulation demands spatial grounding, task-aware reasoning, and precise control. Learning such capabilities becomes particularly challenging in the low-data regime. Prior methods often trade off scalable task-level reasoning and explicit physical structure: video-based approaches can drift geometrically over long horizons, 3D approaches often require depth sensing, and many flow/trajectory interfaces emphasize motion without an explicit RGB-only geometric representation. We introduce SSI-Policy, a modular framework built around a Structured Scene Interface (SSI) -- a unified, RGB-only intermediate representation that jointly encodes monocular depth features, language-grounded object layouts, and instruction-conditioned 2D motion trajectories. Critically, SSI is robot-agnostic and trainable from action-free video, decoupling perception from control so that the downstream policy can learn from few demonstrations. On the LIBERO benchmark with only 10 demonstrations per task, SSI-Policy improves over the strongest prior method by nearly 15\% and remains competitive with 50-demo methods that leverage large-scale external pretraining. Ablations show that geometric and motion cues provide complementary benefits within the shared interface. We further validate on 13 real-world tasks spanning spatial reasoning, cross-embodiment transfer, and contact-rich manipulation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型微调时对所有时间步施加相同的动作监督，缺乏对操作阶段和夹爪事件转换的结构化指导，导致失败集中在夹爪事件转换的关键时刻。

Method: StaKe从演示数据的夹爪状态中自动提取两个辅助信号：阶段分类器识别当前操作阶段，关键帧预测器估计下一个夹爪事件转换时的目标关节动作。两者作为轻量级辅助头在训练时增强表征学习，不改变基础VLA策略架构和推理流程。

Result: 在双臂仿真和单臂Franka真实机器人任务上，StaKe分别取得14%和56%的相对成功率提升，在更长程、更多夹爪事件转换的任务上提升更显著。

Conclusion: 结构化监督是增强VLA模型在长程操作任务中微调的有效且通用策略，StaKe框架无需人工标注即可自动提取有价值的辅助监督信号。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLA模型在机器人操作中的微调，提出StaKe框架通过阶段分类器和关键帧预测器增强动作监督，属于具身智能中机器人操作与策略学习范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation fine-tuning, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world_model, robotic manipulation, fine-tuning, auxiliary supervision, long-horizon tasks

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for generalizable robotic manipulation. During fine-tuning, however, action supervision applies equally across all timesteps, without structured supervision on which manipulation stage the robot is in or what the next gripper-event target should be. This causes failures to concentrate around challenging gripper-event transitions. To address this, we propose StaKe, a plug-in auxiliary supervision framework that automatically derives two complementary signals from demonstration gripper states without manual annotation: a stage classifier that identifies the current manipulation stage, and a keyframe predictor that estimates the target joint action at the next gripper transition. Both are modeled as lightweight auxiliary heads that enrich the learned representations during training, while leaving the base VLA policy architecture and inference loop unchanged. Experiments on bimanual simulation and single-arm Franka real-robot tasks show that StaKe consistently improves success rates (relative gains of 14% and 56%, respectively), with larger improvements on longer-horizon tasks that involve more gripper-event transitions. Ablation studies validate each design choice, and qualitative analysis confirms that the learned representations faithfully track manipulation stages. These results indicate that structured supervision is an effective and general strategy for enhancing VLA fine-tuning in long-horizon manipulation. Project website:

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出一个成本感知的选择性推理框架：(1) 轻量级RGB-生理学学生模型，结合车内视觉观测与窗口级HR/EDA信号；(2) 学习门控机制，决定何时接受快速预测或放弃以进行安全干预；(3) 紧凑的驾驶员状态世界模型模块，用于展开潜在驾驶员状态特征，估计未来快速模型误差和反事实系统级动作成本。

Result: RGB-生理学学生模型在驾驶员需求识别任务上达到0.7440 Macro-F1和0.9099平衡准确率，仅11.39M参数和3.08ms推理延迟。成本感知选择性推理将不安全假阴性率从始终快速推理的17.37%降至约5%（跨种子平均），同时保持部署级延迟。

Conclusion: 可靠的边缘端驾驶员监控不仅需要提升感知骨干网络，还需要推进风险感知的选择性控制和群体鲁棒校准。驾驶员状态世界模型提供了有价值的预测信号，但最差组评估显示存在持续的操作点校准漂移问题。

Classification reason: 论文提出驾驶员状态世界模型（driver-state world modeling），用于预测未来驾驶员状态特征和估计系统级动作成本，属于具身智能/机器人场景中的世界模型，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Applies world modeling to driver monitoring, but the application domain is far from the user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models and generative video. Signals: world model for driver state, selective inference framework, low-latency multimodal monitoring, risk-aware gating, domain mismatch with user priorities

Abstract: Continuous driver monitoring in automated vehicles requires low-latency inference while avoiding unsafe decisions under uncertain driver states. Large vision-language models provide broad multimodal priors, but their latency and limited reliability in this setting make them unsuitable as always-on in-cabin monitors. We propose a cost-aware selective inference framework for deployable multimodal driver monitoring. The core system is a lightweight RGB-physiological student that combines in-cabin visual observations with window-level HR/EDA signals, and a learned gate that decides when to accept the fast prediction or abstain for safety intervention. Additional controls show that the learned scores contain sample-level information beyond scenario priors, while exact physiological synchronization remains a limitation. To incorporate predictive evidence, we further study a compact driver-state world modeling module that rolls out latent driver-state features and estimates future fast-model errors and counterfactual system-level action costs. On scenario-induced driver-demand recognition, the RGB-physiological student improves over RGB-only and physiology-only baselines, reaching 0.7440 Macro-F1 and 0.9099 balanced accuracy with 11.39M parameters and 3.08ms inference latency. Cost-aware selective inference reduces unsafe false negatives from 17.37% under always-fast inference to approximately 5% across seeds, while maintaining deployment-level latency. While driver-state world modeling offers valuable predictive signals, worst-group evaluations highlight persistent operating-point calibration drift. Ultimately, reliable edge driver monitoring requires advancing not only perception backbones, but also risk-aware selective control and group-robust calibration.

</details>

*Mingyang Lyu, Yinqian Sun, Yiyang Jia, Sicheng Shen, Moquan Sha, Huangrui Li, Feifei Zhao, Yi Zeng*

TL;DR: ForesightSafety-VLA 是一个面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的统一诊断性安全评测基准，定义了13类安全分类体系，涵盖物理交互安全、指令安全和视觉感知安全，并通过场景结构、语言指令和视觉观测三个维度的受控变化来诊断故障来源，揭示了当前VLA模型在安全性上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能中VLA模型正朝着通用任务能力发展，但其安全性边界尚不明确。现有评测主要关注任务成功率，缺乏对安全性的系统评估和故障来源的诊断能力。

Method: 定义了13类安全分类体系（Safe-Core物理交互安全、Safe-Lang指令安全、Safe-Vis感知安全），引入累积安全成本（CC）和风险暴露时间（RET）作为过程级风险度量，以及安全/不安全的成功/失败四象限分解。在RoboTwin平台上构建了66个安全增强基础场景，涵盖5种具身形态。

Result: 即使最强的VLA策略也存在不可忽视的安全成本和危险的成功行为；场景结构和视觉变化比语言变化导致更严重的安全退化；安全性紧密耦合于感知、接地和控制能力，不能简化为事后安全过滤。

Conclusion: VLA模型的安全性需要从系统层面进行诊断性评估，安全与感知、接地和控制能力紧密耦合，不能仅靠后处理安全过滤解决。

Classification reason: 该论文研究具身智能中VLA模型的安全性评测，属于机器人/具身智能领域的安全评估，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）方向高度相关，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Safety benchmark for VLA models is valuable but peripheral to your core focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: VLA safety benchmark, diagnostic failure analysis, process-level risk metrics, embodied safety taxonomy, perception-grounding coupling

Abstract: In embodied intelligence, safety is a prerequisite for reliable robot deployment in the physical world. Current vision-language-action (VLA) models continue to advance toward general-purpose task capability, yet their embodied safety limits remain poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce ForesightSafety-VLA, a diagnostic benchmark that makes safety the primary evaluation target for VLA systems. We define a 13-category safety taxonomy covering physical interaction safety (Safe-Core), instruction-side safety (Safe-Lang), and perception-side safety (Safe-Vis), and evaluate policies under three controlled dimensions of variation -- scene structure, language command, and visual observation -- so that failure sources can be diagnosed rather than hidden in a single aggregate score. Beyond binary task success, ForesightSafety-VLA measures process-level risk through cumulative safety cost (CC) and risk exposure time (RET), together with a four-quadrant decomposition of safe/unsafe success and failure. We instantiate 66 safety-augmented base scenarios in RoboTwin across 5 embodiments and report results on representative VLA baselines. Across the evaluated baselines, even the strongest policy incurs non-trivial safety cost and unsafe nominal success, while structure and visual variation induce substantially stronger safety degradation than ordinary language variation. These results suggest that embodied safety is tightly coupled to perception, grounding, and control competence rather than being reducible to post-hoc safety filtering alone.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人感知方法多依赖单一模态（如RGB图像），在光照变化、传感器退化等恶劣条件下鲁棒性不足。同时，多模态自监督学习面临传感器异构（不同表示、频率、延迟和噪声）的挑战，缺乏统一的高质量多模态机器人数据集和高效的表征学习方法。

Method: 构建OctoSense多模态传感器平台（立体RGB+事件相机、LiDAR、热成像、IMU、RTK-GPS、CAN总线/关节角度），采集59小时驾驶数据。提出"late-fusion"掩码自编码器架构：为每种模态设计特定的tokenizer以适配其时空特性，在推理时缓存模态特定token以支持异步新测量输入，通过跨模态掩码重建进行自监督学习。

Result: 在NVIDIA 5090和Orin NX上分别仅需6.68ms和112ms计算表征；在光流、深度、语义分割和自运动估计（平移、旋转、转向角）任务上优于现有纯图像基础模型；在夜间或传感器数据退化场景下仍能保持鲁棒预测性能。

Conclusion: OctoSense展示了多模态自监督学习在真实机器人感知中的有效性，通过异构传感器融合和高效架构设计，在多种感知任务上取得优于单模态基础模型的效果，并在恶劣条件下保持鲁棒性，为机器人多模态感知提供了有价值的开源平台和数据集。

Classification reason: 该论文提出OctoSense多模态传感器平台和数据集，并基于masked autoencoder进行多模态自监督学习，用于机器人感知任务（光流、深度、语义分割、自运动估计）。这属于面向机器人/具身智能的感知与表征学习，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度相关。

Signals: Self-supervised multimodal robot perception, Late-fusion masked autoencoder, 59-hour driving dataset with diverse sensors, Fast inference on edge hardware, Robust to sensor degradation

Abstract: We present OctoSense, an open-source sensor platform with stereo RGB and event cameras, LiDAR, a thermal camera, an inertial measurement unit, RTK-corrected global positioning system, and proprioception (CAN bus data from a car, and joint angles for a quadruped robot). The eponymous OctoSense dataset contains 59 hours of time-synchronized driving data across different types of environments at different times of the day, including situations with highly degraded sensors. We demonstrate multi-modal self-supervised learning using such real-world robotics data, where sensors have different representations, frequencies, latencies and noise. Our approach, a "late-fusion" masked autoencoder, (i) uses modality-specific tokenizers to account for different spatiotemporal characteristics of these sensors, and (ii) caches modality-specific tokens at inference time to process new measurements as they come. This architecture (i) is fast (6.68 ms and 112 ms on NVIDIA 5090 and Orin NX respectively, to compute the representation), (ii) performs better than existing image-only foundation models on tasks such as estimation of optical flow, depth, semantic segmentation, and ego-motion (translation, rotation, and steering angle), and (iii) predicts robustly at nighttime or in situations where sensory data is degraded. See our project page for links to the dataset, code, and supplementary videos: .

TL;DR: 本文提出PAMAE，一种即插即用的阶段感知混合专家动作模块，用于改进流匹配视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在多阶段机器人操作中的动作生成可靠性。通过引入阶段感知路由器和两阶段训练，PAMAE在仿真任务上相比强基线提升了最高9.2%的任务成功率。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配VLA策略使用单一共享动作专家，难以捕捉多阶段操作任务中不同执行阶段的特定控制模式，导致动作生成可靠性不足。

Method: 提出PAMAE模块，将原始流匹配动作专家替换为稀疏专家混合，包含：(1) 阶段感知路由器，利用执行阶段线索分配专家；(2) 轻量级阶段预测头；(3) 路由对齐目标。采用两阶段训练：先以标准流匹配损失预热专家模块，再在辅助监督下优化阶段一致路由。

Result: 在多阶段操作仿真任务上，PAMAE相比强VLA基线提升最高9.2%的任务成功率。消融实验表明阶段监督路由和分阶段优化对性能提升均不可或缺。

Conclusion: 阶段一致的专家分配是提升流匹配VLA策略可靠性和动作质量的有效机制，PAMAE作为即插即用模块具有良好实用性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向多阶段机器人操作的VLA策略，使用flow-matching和MoE进行动作生成，属于具身智能中的机器人动作规划与控制，因此映射到[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制。

Abstract: Reliable action generation for multi-stage robotic manipulation remains challenging for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. While existing flow-matching VLA policies offer strong multimodal grounding and generalization, they typically employ a single shared action expert, limiting their ability to capture phase-specific control patterns across distinct execution stages. We propose a plug-and-play Phase-Aware Mixture-of-Experts Action Module (PAMAE), as a step towards more reliable phase-consistent action generation. PAMAE replaces the original flow-matching action expert with a sparse expert mixture while preserving the pretrained VLA backbone. PAMAE introduces a phase-aware router that leverages execution-phase cues to allocate action generation across experts, supported by a lightweight phase prediction head and a routing alignment objective. To stabilize specialization, we adopt a two-stage training scheme that first warms up the expert module under the standard flow-matching loss and then optimizes phase-consistent routing under auxiliary supervision. On multi-stage manipulation simulation tasks, PAMAE improves task success by up to \textbf{9.2\%} over strong VLA baselines. Further ablations show that both phase-supervised routing and staged optimization are essential for the observed gains. Our results highlight phase-consistent expert allocation as an effective mechanism for improving the reliability and action quality of flow-matching VLA policies.

</details>

*Jiayu Yang, Tao Yang, Weijun Li, Xiang Chang, Fei Chao, Changjing Shang, Qiang Shen*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程机器人操作对物理不可行的状态转移、接触引起的扰动以及执行过程中缺乏有效的自我纠错高度敏感。现有 VLA 模型以前馈方式生成动作，缺乏显式的物理可行性检查和在线执行错误诊断，导致长程任务中鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出 PhysReflect-VLA 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Feasibility Operator，评估候选动作是否产生动力学一致的状态转移；(2) Action Explanation Operator，验证状态转移的连贯性；(3) LLM-based Reflection Module，分析状态差异并生成纠错指导。采用两阶段训练流程：先稳定可行性建模，再将反思集成到控制循环中。

Result: 在多阶段、接触丰富的真实机器人操作任务上，相比代表性 VLA 基线，PhysReflect-VLA 在阶段稳定性和整体任务成功率上取得一致提升，平均增益 5.4%。消融实验表明可行性检查和基于反思的纠错均对执行鲁棒性有贡献。

Conclusion: 嵌入物理一致性检查和在线自反思对于实现可靠的长程机器人操作至关重要，PhysReflect-VLA 为增强 VLA 策略的执行可靠性提供了一种有效的即插即用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PhysReflect-VLA，通过物理可行性评估和自反思机制增强 VLA 策略在长程机器人操作中的可靠性，属于机器人具身策略与规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation reliability, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, physical feasibility, self-reflection, VLA policy, robotic manipulation

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation is highly sensitive to physically infeasible transitions, contact-induced disturbances, and the lack of effective self-correction during execution. Although Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong task grounding through multimodal learning, they typically generate actions in a feed-forward manner without explicitly checking physical feasibility or diagnosing execution errors online. We present PhysReflect-VLA, a plug-and-play execution-time reliability framework that augments VLA policies with physical feasibility evaluation and structured self-reflection in a closed-loop control pipeline. A Feasibility Operator evaluates whether candidate actions induce dynamically consistent state transitions; an Action Explanation Operator verifies transition coherence; and an LLM-based Reflection Module analyzes state discrepancies to generate corrective guidance for subsequent actions. A two-stage training procedure stabilizes feasibility modeling and integrates reflection into the control loop. Experiments on multi-stage, contact-rich real-world manipulation tasks show consistent improvements in stage-wise stability and overall task success compared with representative VLA baselines with an average gain of 5.4\%. Ablation results further indicate that feasibility checking and reflection-based correction both contribute to improved execution robustness. These results highlight the importance of embedding physical consistency checks and online self-reflection for reliable long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>

Abstract: I describe my solution to the LeHome Challenge 2026, an ICRA 2026 competition on bimanual garment folding. The system placed 1st of 62 teams in the online (simulation) round and 2nd in the real-world final. It improves a vision-language-action (VLA) policy with a reinforcement-learning loop. The policy is its own value function: the same network that predicts actions also predicts success, progress, and a few task-relevant future quantities, and those predictions drive advantage estimation, live failure detection, and candidate selection. The work mostly recombines existing RL ideas with engineering and optimization contributions that can be used together as one recipe or individually: AWR + RECAP combined for flow-matching VLA; an asynchronous distributed training / rollout pipeline through HuggingFace Hub; inference-time hyperparameters optimization via Thompson sampling; a sim-to-real recipe with camera-alignment tooling, heavy augmentation and DAgger-like HIL data collection.

</details>

Abstract: Building persistent embodied agents in unstructured environments demands unified orchestration of heterogeneous tools spanning both cyber (APIs, IoT) and physical (manipulation, navigation) domains, coupled with autonomous recovery from physical failures that inevitably arise over extended operation. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: VLM-based planners lack a unified cyber-physical action space, agent frameworks accumulate unbounded context that degrades temporal coherence, and VLA policies execute open-loop without detecting their own failures. We argue that persistent autonomy requires not a monolithic model but a hierarchical asynchronous architecture with explicit separation of planning, memory, and verification. To this end, we present OmniAct, a framework integrating a multimodal semantic planner for skill routing across unified action spaces, an adaptive hierarchical memory with event-boundary-driven compression for sub-linear context growth, and an asynchronous visual preemption engine that closes the semantic loop during physical execution. Across 40 real-world long-horizon tasks on two robotic platforms coordinating four IoT devices, OmniAct achieves consistent improvements in end-to-end success across all complexity levels, maintains near-flat token consumption over under 100k+ accumulated interaction tokens, and elevates mid-scale open-weight models to proprietary-level performance.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: E-TTS通过历史感知的迭代精炼有效统一了具身任务中的推理扩展与动作扩展，验证了测试时扩展在机器人操作中的有效性，且框架具有模块化和即插即用的优势。

Importance reason: Proposes a modular test-time scaling framework for embodied manipulation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: test-time scaling, embodied manipulation, vision-language-action models, iterative refinement, modular framework

Abstract: Recently, a few works have made early attempts to study test-time scaling for embodied tasks. However, two major challenges remain unsolved: (1) reasoning can effectively improve the performance of the policy, but its scaling mechanism has seldom been studied; (2) historical information is essential, as embodied tasks are inherently long-horizon and sequential, making sole reliance on current observations for action scaling inadequate due to the lack of historical context utilization. To address these challenges, we introduce E-TTS, a modular and plug-and-play Embodied Test-Time Scaling framework that unifies reasoning and action scaling for robotic manipulation via history-aware iterative refinement with vision-language verifiers. To support joint reasoning-action scaling, E-TTS performs reasoning-action joint sampling and scoring in a pairwise manner. To better utilize historical information, E-TTS uses a history buffer to store historical context, which is then used by reasoning and action verifiers to evaluate the sampled candidates. Unlike conventional open-loop TTS methods, E-TTS introduces feedback generation into the sampling process to form a closed-loop iterative refinement mechanism, enhancing both inference efficiency and environmental adaptability. Each component functions as an independent and composable module, allowing flexible and adaptive configuration depending on task requirements. To evaluate the advantages of our framework, we conduct experiments across 4 different benchmarks, 6 environments, 3 embodiments, and 4 base vision-language-action models. The experimental results demonstrate that, without requiring additional expert data collection or retraining, E-TTS consistently improves performance, achieving up to a 33.14% increase in simulation and 26.62% in real-world scenarios.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中部署的机器人很少在单一固定的动力学模型下运行——风况变化、负载变化、电池消耗、接触变化和硬件磨损都会改变动力学特性。然而大多数基于学习的控制器仅训练一次便部署，无法利用部署经验进一步提升任务性能。本文旨在解决这一问题，使机器人能够利用真实世界经验持续改进策略。

Method: 提出一个持续学习框架：(1) 结合解析物理先验与神经残差，从真实状态-动作轨迹中学习条件感知的动力学模型；(2) 使用循环编码器从近期交互中推断当前隐条件，该条件同时调节残差模型和策略；(3) 通过可微仿真，利用从潜模型采样的多样化动力学进行策略学习；(4) 部署时，用在线推断的条件替换采样条件，使策略通过识别而非残差重拟合来恢复重复性动力学。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，该方法在多种未观测扰动下提升了策略性能。在真实四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中，策略在约1秒内恢复对重复性扰动的适应，比在线残差重拟合快约5倍；大扰动下的悬停和跟踪误差分别降低65.7%和53.3%。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过变分神经动力学模型学习条件感知的动力学，并结合可微仿真进行策略学习，能够有效利用部署经验提升机器人策略在变化动力学下的性能。该方法通过识别而非重拟合来恢复重复性扰动，显著加快了适应速度。

Importance reason: Strong robotics world model paper but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: world model, continual learning, neural dynamics, differentiable simulation, quadrotor control

Abstract: Robots deployed in the real world rarely operate under a single fixed dynamics model: wind changes, payloads vary, batteries drain, contacts shift, and hardware wears. Yet most learning-based controllers are trained once and deployed as if learning were complete. This prevents the robot from using deployment experience to further improve task performance. In this work, we propose a continual learning framework that uses real-world experience to improve robot policies under hidden and recurring dynamics. Our method learns a condition-aware dynamics model from real state-action trajectories by combining an analytical physics prior with a neural residual for unmodeled effects. A recurrent encoder infers the current hidden condition from recent interaction, and this estimate conditions both the residual model and the policy. Policy learning is performed via differentiable simulation using diverse learned dynamics sampled from the latent model. At deployment, these sampled conditions are replaced by conditions inferred online from recent real interaction, allowing the policy to recover recurring dynamics by recognition rather than residual re-fitting. Through extensive simulation studies and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that the framework improves policy performance under diverse unobserved disturbances. On real quadrotor trajectory tracking under changing wind, the policy recovers from recurring disturbances in roughly 1s, about 5x faster than online residual re-fitting. It also reduces large-disturbance hover and tracking errors by 65.7% and 53.3% over the state-of-the-art online adaptation approaches

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN) 框架，利用 World Action Model (WAM) 生成伪回放轨迹，使机器人策略在不存储原始人类演示的情况下持续学习新任务，在仿真和真实实验中减少高达50%的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人持续学习面临灾难性遗忘问题，传统方法需要存储大量原始演示数据用于回放，存在存储开销和隐私限制。作者希望利用世界模型的生成能力，在不存储原始数据的情况下实现有效的持续模仿学习。

Method: 提出 REGEN 框架，基于 World Action Model (WAM) 递归生成伪回放轨迹。WAM 不仅能预测机器人动作，还能生成未来的视觉观测。在持续适应过程中，REGEN 仅基于先前的任务指令和当前任务观测，递归查询 WAM 合成伪回放轨迹，让策略在合成数据上复习已学任务。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作实验中，REGEN 相比顺序微调减少了高达50%的灾难性遗忘，性能接近需要访问真实回放数据的特权经验回放方法。同时识别了长时序视觉退化与动作-观测不一致性作为生成回放的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: WAM 作为无需存储演示的持续机器人学习基础具有广阔前景，REGEN 有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，但长时序生成质量仍需改进。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 World Action Model (WAM) 用于机器人持续模仿学习，通过生成伪回放轨迹来缓解灾难性遗忘。论文核心是面向机器人操作的世界模型，属于 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 范畴。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continual learning for robotics, aligning with high-priority world_model direction and video generation world model subtopic. Signals: world action model, continual imitation learning, generative replay, catastrophic forgetting reduction, real-world robot experiments

Abstract: Going beyond predicting robot actions, World Action Models (WAMs) can also generate future visual observations. We build on this generative capability to propose Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a continual imitation learning framework that synthesizes pseudo-replay trajectories, enabling a robot policy to rehearse previously learned tasks without storing their original human demonstrations. During continual adaptation, REGEN recursively queries the WAM to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories conditioned only on prior task instructions and current-task observations. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation settings show that REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to $50\%$ relative to sequential fine-tuning, while approaching the performance of privileged experience replay methods that require access to real replay data. Finally, we analyze the factors limiting generated replay, identifying long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as the primary bottlenecks. Our results establish WAMs as a promising foundation for continual robot learning without stored demonstrations.

</details>

Classification reason: 该论文提出从视频中自监督学习隐式3D物理动力学，将物理建模为时空状态平流问题，属于物理世界建模与神经模拟器方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous 3D representation learning from video, aligning with top user priorities. Signals: world model, neural simulator, 3D physics from video, self-supervised, volumetric latent space

Abstract: We present a self-supervised framework for learning implicit 3D physical dynamics directly from video-derived supervisory signals. While current generative video models achieve high visual fidelity, they lack a 3D geometric foundation, often resulting in physical inconsistencies and a failure to maintain object permanence. We address this by shifting the predictive bottleneck from 2D image space to a `lifted' 3D Volumetric Latent Space. Our method unprojects semantic features from a Video Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) into a voxelized grid, grounded by monocular depth priors. This lifting enables a Volumetric Feature Advection to learn an action-conditioned transition operator that treats physics as a spatio-temporal state advection problem, i.e., learn implicit 3D physics. Unlike state-of-the-art hybrid models that rely on explicit classical simulators for training and/or inference, our architecture tracks material states implicitly within high-dimensional V-JEPA features. This allows for the emergent simulation of heterogeneous phenomena (e.g., rigid body motion in fluid flow) within a single, unified pipeline. Supervised solely via end-to-end video-derived signal plus action conditions, without access to physics engine internal states, labels, or surrogate models, our model demonstrates good long-term structural stability and physical plausibility on multiple benchmarks (CLEVERER, PhysInOne, PhysGaia). We believe that this work opens a scalable pathway toward general-purpose dynamic world models that internalize the 3D invariants of the physical world solely through passive observation of monocular videos.

</details>


### [24] [LithoDreamer: A Physics-Informed World Model for Multi-Stage Computational Lithography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26713)
*Yuqi Jiang, Yumeng Liu, Zimu Li, Jinyuan Deng, Qian Jin, Yucheng Cui, Yu Li, Xunzhao Yin, Qi Sun, Cheng Zhuo*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算光刻模型无法捕捉从掩模到最终显影图像（ADI）的完整连续物理过程，缺乏对多阶段物理演化的统一建模能力。

Method: 将光刻流程建模为"版图→掩模→光刻胶图像→显影后图像"的多步状态演化系统，学习阶段特定的物理信息潜空间，通过对比变分优化范式在无连续监督下实现可解释的干预优化与状态转移。

Conclusion: LithoDreamer 验证了世界模型框架在计算光刻这一工业级物理过程中的有效性，为半导体制造中的物理仿真提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LithoDreamer，一个用于计算光刻的物理信息世界模型，将光刻过程建模为多步状态演化系统，属于物理世界建模与神经仿真器范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 可交互与物理世界建模 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong physics-informed world model for lithography, but domain-specific to semiconductor manufacturing, not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: world model, physics-informed, computational lithography, contrastive variational optimization, multi-step evolution

Abstract: As semiconductor technology nodes scale, computational lithography is essential for ensuring yield and performance. However, lithography is a continuous physical process involving mask optimization, optical imaging, resist exposure, and development, which existing models fail to capture. To overcome this limitation, we present LithoDreamer, the first physics-informed World Model (WM) framework for computational lithography, which formulates the ``Layout-Mask-Resist Image-After Development Image (ADI)'' pipeline as a decision-driven multi-step evolution system. LithoDreamer captures feature changes between adjacent states to model stage-specific physics-informed latent spaces, in which it controls process intervention exploration and drives subsequent state transitions. To achieve interpretable intervention optimization without continuous supervision, we propose a contrastive variational optimization paradigm that contrasts the latent differences between intervention paths with variational evolution constraints, guiding the model to generate evolutions consistent with real lithography physics. Experiments show LithoDreamer achieves state-of-the-art performance in forward evolution and inverse planning. Our lithography dataset is publicly available at GitHub ( ).

</details>

*Yiming Chen, Yushi Lan, Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PhysiFormer 是一个扩散 Transformer，直接在3D世界坐标中对物体顶点轨迹进行去噪扩散，无需刚体约束或因果归纳偏置，即可生成物理合理的刚体/弹性体运动，并泛化到未见几何形状和混合材料场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在像素空间操作，受视角依赖限制且难以保证物理一致性；而传统神经物理方法依赖特化潜空间或显式施加刚体/因果归纳偏置。作者希望探索一种无需强归纳偏置、直接在3D世界坐标中进行物理模拟的扩散方法。

Method: PhysiFormer 将物体表示为3D网格顶点，输入初始顶点位置、速度和材料类型（刚体/弹性体），通过扩散 Transformer 直接预测未来顶点轨迹。模型采用分解注意力机制（时间×空间×物体），支持排列不变的多物体推理，无需显式物体编码。训练数据为超过10万条模拟轨迹。

Result: PhysiFormer 在轨迹精度、刚体保持和动量一致性上显著优于自回归基线；能泛化到混合材料、未见真实世界几何形状和更多物体数量的场景；概率公式化使其能生成多种合理未来轨迹。

Conclusion: 坐标空间扩散是迈向视角不变、几何感知的世界建模的有前景方向，对机器人、图形学和物理设计具有潜在价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PhysiFormer，在3D世界坐标空间中对物体力学运动进行扩散建模，属于物理世界建模与神经仿真器范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous multimodal representation via diffusion in 3D world space, aligning with top-weighted priority subtopics. Signals: world model, diffusion transformer, 3D vertex trajectory diffusion, no rigid body inductive bias, generalizes to unseen geometries

Abstract: We present PhysiFormer, a diffusion transformer for physically-plausible 3D object motion. Unlike video world models that operate in view-dependent pixel space, PhysiFormer represents objects as 3D meshes expressed in world coordinates. Given the initial vertex positions and velocities, as well as object material type, rigid or elastic, the model samples future vertex trajectories. While related neural physics approaches build on ad-hoc latent spaces or explicitly enforce rigidity and causality, PhysiFormer shows that excellent results can be obtained without any such inductive biases, by casting vertex trajectory prediction as a single denoising diffusion process directly in world coordinates. The probabilistic formulation captures uncertainty in the learned dynamics, enabling diverse plausible futures from initial conditions, making this framework potentially useful for applications with unobserved uncertainty. The model features attention factorised over time, space, and objects for efficiency, enabling permutation-invariant multi-object reasoning without needing explicit object encoding. Trained on over 100k simulated trajectories, PhysiFormer generates rigid and elastic mechanics, and generalises to mixed-material settings, unseen real-world geometries, and larger object counts. It substantially outperforms autoregressive baselines in trajectory accuracy, rigidity preservation, and momentum-based physical consistency. Our results position coordinate-space diffusion as a promising step toward view-invariant, geometry-aware world modelling for robotics, graphics, and physical design. Visualisations, code, and models are available at .

</details>


### [26] [Look-Before-Move: Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention in Dynamic 3D Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26964)
*Jiaming Bian, Bingliang Li, Yuehao Wu, Pichao Wang, Zhi Wang, Hailan Ma, Huadong Mo, Zhenhong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Look-Before-Move框架，在动态3D故事世界中实现叙事引导的视觉注意力与相机规划。框架先通过语义观察合约将导演意图转化为可执行约束，再通过蒙特卡洛视点搜索找到符合叙事和几何约束的视点，最后通过语义轨迹连接生成连续、无碰撞的相机运动。在基于StoryBlender构建的50个故事、457个场景、1585个镜头的基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI和世界模型在动态3D环境中运行时，视觉感知需要从被动解释给定观察转向主动决定观察什么。现有方法在相机规划中缺乏对"移动前先看"这一主动视觉注意力的建模，即相机不仅要生成平滑运动，还要决定在移动前获取哪些视觉证据。

Method: 提出Look-Before-Move框架，包含三个模块：(1) 语义观察合约(Semantic Observation Contract)将导演意图转化为可执行的视觉约束；(2) 蒙特卡洛视点搜索(Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search)寻找符合叙事和几何约束的视点；(3) 语义轨迹连接(Semantic Trajectory Grounding)将选定视点连接为连续、无碰撞、时序一致的相机运动。同时构建了基于StoryBlender的动态3D故事世界基准。

Result: 在50个故事、457个场景、1585个镜头的基准上，Look-Before-Move在主体感知、意图一致性和轨迹质量方面均优于代表性基线方法，验证了在生成相机运动之前组织视觉注意力的重要性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在动态3D环境中，先进行叙事引导的视觉注意力规划再执行相机运动是有效的。Look-Before-Move框架通过分离观察规范与运动执行，实现了更符合叙事意图和物理约束的相机规划，为具身AI和世界模型中的主动视觉感知提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究动态3D故事世界中的视觉注意力与相机规划，属于可交互与物理世界建模范畴。论文构建了动态3D故事世界基准，提出在物理3D约束下进行叙事引导的视觉注意力与相机运动规划，与[interactive_physical_world]（可交互与物理世界建模）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and narrative-driven visual attention, but lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, dynamic 3D environments, narrative-grounded attention, camera planning, StoryBlender benchmark

Abstract: As embodied AI and world models increasingly operate in dynamic 3D environments, visual perception must move beyond passively interpreting given observations toward actively deciding what to observe. We study this problem through camera planning in dynamic 3D story worlds, where the camera must not only generate smooth motion, but also decide what visual evidence should be acquired before it moves. We formulate this capability as Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention, where the camera acts as an embodied observer that determines what to observe, how to compose the observation, and how to shift attention over time under narrative intent and physical 3D constraints. To realize this capability, we propose Look-Before-Move, a camera planning framework that separates observation specification from motion execution. It first builds a Semantic Observation Contract to convert directorial intent into executable visual constraints, then performs Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search to find narrative-compliant and geometrically feasible viewpoints, and finally applies Semantic Trajectory Grounding to connect selected viewpoints into continuous, collision-aware, and temporally coherent camera motion. We further construct a dynamic 3D Story World Benchmark based on StoryBlender, covering 50 stories, 457 scenes, and 1585 shots with animated characters, semantic scene configurations, and executable 3D environments. Experiments show that our framework improves subject perception, intent consistency, and trajectory quality over representative baselines, demonstrating the importance of organizing visual attention before generating camera motion.

</details>


### [27] [A Generalization Theory for JEPA-Based World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27014)
*Jingyi Cui, Qi Zhang, Hongwei Wen, Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (97.15) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了 JEPA 世界模型的泛化理论，将 JEPA 预训练形式化为条件谱图学习问题，证明其等价于动作条件共现矩阵的低秩分解，并建立了预训练误差与下游规划遗憾之间的泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 作为世界模型在经验上取得了成功，但其理论基础尚不明确，缺乏对潜空间预测模型相比输入级预测模型优势与局限的理论理解。

Method: 将 JEPA 预训练建模为条件谱图学习问题，证明 JEPA 目标等价于动作条件共现矩阵的低秩分解，并建立预训练误差与下游规划遗憾之间的泛化界。

Result: 推导出 JEPA 世界模型的有限样本泛化界，揭示了潜维度在近似误差与样本误差之间的固有权衡。

Conclusion: 为 JEPA 世界模型提供了首个泛化理论，从理论上阐明了潜空间预测模型相比输入级预测模型的优势（更好的泛化）与局限（近似误差），为理解世界模型的设计原则提供了理论指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）作为世界模型的理论基础，属于世界模型方向。JEPA 在潜空间中进行预测动力学建模，与可交互物理世界建模中的环境动力学建模高度相关，因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic "interactive_physical_world"。

Importance reason: First generalization theory for JEPA world models directly supports your high-priority world model and continuous representation subtopics. Signals: world model theory, JEPA generalization bound, conditional spectral graph learning, latent predictive models, planning regret

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for world modeling by learning predictive dynamics in a latent space rather than generating future observations at the input level. Despite their empirical success, the theoretical understanding of JEPA-based world models remains limited. In this paper, we develop the first generalization theory for JEPA-based world models. We formulate JEPA pretraining as a conditional spectral graph learning problem and show that the JEPA objective is equivalent to a low-rank factorization of an action-conditioned co-occurrence matrix. Building on this characterization, we establish a connection between JEPA pretraining error and downstream planning regret, leading to a finite-sample generalization bound for JEPA-based world models. Our analysis reveals an inherent trade-off between approximation and sample errors with respect to the latent dimension, providing theoretical insights into the advantages and limitations of latent predictive models compared with input-level predictive approaches.

</details>


### [28] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang, Liam Wang, Peng-Chen Chen, Min Seop Kwak, Seungryong Kim, Brian Bell, Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.AI, math.NA

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 值得关注 (68.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS)，一种将 PDE 残差场作为网络输入、通过迭代学习更新策略来修正预测的神经求解器，在多个 PDE 族上取得显著精度提升（湍流 Kolmogorov 流上提升达 10 倍），且具备零样本跨参数和跨方程泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代理模型将 PDE 求解视为纯统计任务，训练后无法修正自身约束违反并难以外推；混合方法通过梯度下降最小化 PDE 残差来提升物理正确性，但继承了经典优化器的计算开销和不稳定性。本文理论证明在病态系统中最小化 PDE 残差并非重建精度的可靠代理，因此需要一种更高效、更鲁棒的神经求解范式。

Method: ENS 的核心思想是将 PDE 残差场作为网络的直接输入（而非优化目标），使网络能够读取自身误差的空间结构，并学习一个迭代更新策略来逐步修正预测。在每个迭代步，网络接收当前预测和对应的残差场，输出修正量，从而形成自纠正的迭代过程。

Result: 在四个 PDE 族（包括湍流 Kolmogorov 流）上，ENS 在绝大多数设置中取得了最高预测精度，在湍流 Kolmogorov 流上精度提升达 10 倍，同时避免了混合方法的高计算成本。ENS 的修正策略在分布偏移下具有泛化能力，包括零样本参数变化和跨方程迁移，在病态区域优势最为显著。

Conclusion: ENS 通过将 PDE 残差作为输入而非优化目标，实现了高效、可泛化的神经 PDE 求解，在病态系统中显著优于依赖残差最小化的混合方法，为物理世界建模和神经仿真器提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出基于误差条件的神经求解器（ENS），用于求解偏微分方程（PDE），属于物理世界建模与神经仿真器范畴，与 canonical subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel error-conditioned neural solver for PDEs with strong zero-shot generalization, directly relevant to world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world_model, neural simulator, efficient inference, zero-shot generalization, PDE solving

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: .

</details>

### [29] [PhysEditWorld: A Large-Scale Dataset Toward Physics-Editable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26694)
*Bin Hu, Yanwen Ma, Jiehui Huang, Ziliang Zhang, Haoning Wu, Ruicheng Zhang, Yaokun Li, Zijun Wang, Yuechen Zhang, Chun-Mei Tseng, Hanhui Li, Shengju Qian, Jun Zhou, Kaipeng Zhang, Xiaodan Liang, Jiaya Jia, Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PhysEditWorld是一个大规模多模态数据集，基于UE5 replay-and-rendering流水线构建，包含12个电影级场景、100+小时游戏交互、6000万+帧渲染数据，每个样本提供RGB、深度、法线、音频、动作轨迹、相机轨迹、引擎状态、语义标注和显式重力标签。该数据集支持可控物理编辑的世界模型研究，初步实验表明其能提升重力忠实动力学建模和物理编辑一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏世界模型虽然能生成视觉上合理的动作条件视频，但其交互行为通常局限于探索性轨迹，物理动力学仅作为数据中的隐式相关性被学习，而非可控变量。这限制了它们在需要显式操控物理规则（如重力）的游戏环境中的应用。因此，作者希望构建一个带有显式物理参数标注的大规模多模态数据集，以支持可控物理编辑的世界模型研究。

Method: 基于UE5引擎实现replay-and-rendering流水线：记录归一化的动作轨迹，然后在多种重力配置下回放相同的初始状态、角色控制器、动作序列和相机策略，从而生成可控且可归因的物理变化。数据集包含12个电影级UE5场景、100+小时游戏交互、6000万+帧渲染数据，每个样本提供RGB、深度、法线、音频、动作轨迹、相机轨迹、引擎状态、语义标注和显式重力标签等多模态信号。

Result: 在生成式视频模型和世界理解模型上的初步效用研究表明：PhysEditWorld能够提升重力忠实动力学建模能力，增强物理编辑下的一致性，为可控世界建模研究提供了可扩展的基础。

Conclusion: PhysEditWorld作为首个带有显式物理参数（重力）标注的大规模多模态数据集，填补了现有世界模型数据集在可控物理编辑方面的空白。它为未来可控世界建模、物理感知视频生成和交互式模拟器研究提供了重要资源。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了PhysEditWorld数据集，包含物理参数（重力）标注的多模态视频数据，用于可控世界建模研究。这属于世界模型方向下的数据集贡献，且world_model方向下已有generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）这一非规范但已存在的子主题，因此复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Directly supports world model and video generation research with a large-scale physics-editable dataset, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: world model, video generation, controllable generation, large-scale dataset, physics-editable

Abstract: Recent game world models can synthesize visually plausible, action-conditioned rollouts. However, their interaction behaviors often remain limited to exploratory or wandering trajectories, and physical dynamics are typically learned as implicit correlations from data rather than as controllable variables. This limitation hinders their applicability to authored game environments, where physical rules are deliberately designed and require explicit manipulation. We introduce PhysEditWorld, a multimodal dataset with physical parameters, with a primary focus on gravity in this initial version. At its core, PhysEditWorld is built upon a replay paradigm implemented with a UE5 replay-and-rendering pipeline. Each scenario records a normalized action trace and replays the same initial state, character controller, action sequence, and camera policy under multiple gravity configurations, enabling controlled and attributable physical variation. PhysEditWorld contains 12 cinematic UE5 scenes, over 100 hours of gameplay interactions, and more than 60 million rendered rollout frames. Each sample provides synchronized multimodal signals, including RGB, depth, normals, audio, action traces, camera trajectory, engine states, semantic annotations, and explicit gravity labels. We further conduct initial utility studies on both generative video models and world understanding models, demonstrating that PhysEditWorld enables improved gravity-faithful dynamics modeling, enhances consistency under physical edits, and provides a scalable foundation for controllable world modeling research.

</details>

### [30] [Fast LeWorldModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26217)
*Yuntian Gao, Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.RO

TL;DR: Fast-LeWM 提出一种基于动作前缀预测的快速潜空间世界模型，通过并行预测多个前缀对应的未来潜状态，替代了传统自回归 rollout，在提升规划成功率的同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWorldModel (LeWM) 等 JEPA 世界模型在视觉规划中需要反复应用单步潜状态转移模型来 rollout 候选动作序列，导致计算成本高且长程预测中累积潜空间误差。作者希望设计一种更高效的潜空间世界模型，避免逐自回归展开。

Method: 提出 Fast-LeWM，将动作前缀作为基本预测单元。给定当前潜状态和候选动作序列，模型编码其所有前缀，并行预测每个前缀执行后到达的未来潜状态。前缀级监督迫使模型学习不同动作前缀下状态的连续演化，而非仅拟合单步转移。规划时，直接使用编码动作序列的最后一个前缀 token 评估对应未来潜状态，无需逐中间状态 rollout。

Result: 在多个任务上，Fast-LeWM 相比 LeWM 提升了平均成功率，同时显著减少了规划时间。开环潜损失更低，且随 rollout 步数增长的速度明显更慢。

Conclusion: 动作前缀预测是一种有效的潜空间世界模型加速方法，能够在不牺牲甚至提升规划性能的前提下大幅降低计算成本，并缓解长程预测中的误差累积问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Fast-LeWM，一种基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的快速潜空间世界模型，用于动作条件未来状态预测与视觉规划。这属于世界模型方向中的视频世界模型（video_world_model）子主题，因为其核心是潜空间中的动作条件未来预测与规划，与视频世界模型的定义高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model efficiency via parallel action-prefix prediction, aligning with top-priority world model and continuous latent modeling. Signals: world model efficiency, action-prefix prediction, parallel latent rollout, reduces autoregressive accumulation, improves planning speed

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), including recent LeWorldModel (LeWM), have become a promising foundation for reconstruction-free visual world models. For visual planning, however, LeWM evaluates candidate action sequences by repeatedly applying a local one-step latent transition model. This autoregressive rollout makes planning computationally expensive and exposes the predicted trajectory to accumulated latent errors as the horizon grows. We propose Fast LeWorldModel (Fast-LeWM), a fast latent world model that replaces repeated local rollout with action-prefix prediction. Given the current latent and a candidate action sequence, Fast-LeWM encodes its prefixes and predicts the future latents reached after executing those prefixes in parallel. By making action prefixes the basic prediction unit, Fast-LeWM directly models action effects accumulated to different extents over multiple horizons. This prefix-level supervision forces the model to learn how states continuously evolve under different action prefixes, rather than only fitting one-step state transitions. During planning, the predictor can use the last prefix token from the encoded action sequence to evaluate the corresponding future latent without explicitly rolling through each intermediate imagined state. Across multiple tasks, Fast-LeWM improves average success over LeWM while substantially reducing planning time, achieving lower open-loop latent loss whose growth becomes significantly slower as the rollout horizon increases.

</details>

*Zizhao Yuan, Zhengtu Liang, Taowen Wang, Qiwei Liang, Yichi Wang, Yunheng Wang, Yuetong Fang, Lusong Li, Zecui Zeng, Renjing Xu*

TL;DR: 本文提出 DexAC-WM，通过结构化动作条件建模（动作 tokenization + 局部细化 + 全局调制）和语义分支（提供物体场景先验），解决了高自由度灵巧手动作条件下世界模型中的动作条件不平衡问题，在 EgoDex 和 EgoVerse 数据集上显著提升了视频预测的视觉真实感和动作跟随一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型通常将整个动作序列压缩为单一表征，这在低自由度控制中有效，但在高自由度灵巧手场景中，动作具有异质性（不同动作分量量级差异大），统一聚合会导致优化不平衡，难以建模细粒度效果。因此需要重新思考高自由度场景下的动作条件建模方式。

Method: 提出 DexAC-WM，包含两个核心设计：(1) DexAC（结构化动作条件模块）：通过动作 tokenization 保留维度级语义，再通过局部细化（local refinement）和全局调制（global modulation）将动作信号与视觉动态对齐；(2) 语义分支（semantic branch）：引入物体-场景先验，增强世界模型对动态视觉细节的捕捉能力。两者结合实现高自由度动作条件视频预测。

Result: 在 EgoDex 和 EgoVerse 数据集上，DexAC-WM 在 FID、FVD 和 PCK 指标上显著优于基线方法，验证了结构化动作建模和语义分支在视觉-时序真实感和动作跟随一致性上的有效性。此外，DexAC 可扩展到其他骨干网络，展示了其可扩展性。

Conclusion: 将世界模型扩展到高自由度控制需要结构化动作建模和语义基础。DexAC-WM 通过动作 tokenization 和语义先验的结合，有效解决了高自由度动作条件下的建模不平衡问题，为灵巧手世界模型提供了新的设计范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出高自由度灵巧手动作条件下的世界模型 DexAC-WM，核心是动作条件视频预测（action-conditioned video prediction），属于视频世界模型范畴，与 [CANONICAL] video_world_model 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model conditioning for high-DoF control, aligning with your priority on video world models and structured action modeling. Signals: world model, dexterous manipulation, action conditioning, video prediction, structured tokenization

Abstract: Recent advances in action-conditioned world models show promising progress in modeling complex interactions and forecasting future states under diverse action sequences. While these models are often driven by stronger visual representations and model capacity, action conditioning itself remains underexplored. Most existing approaches compress the entire action sequence into a single representation, which works well for low-DoF control but becomes less reliable in high-DoF scenarios. We observe that high-DoF dexterous actions are inherently heterogeneous, spanning multiple orders of magnitude, where large-scale motions coexist with subtle but important signals. When uniformly aggregated, optimization exhibits an imbalance across action components, which hinders the modeling of fine-grained effects and affects action fidelity. We therefore propose DexAC-WM, which treats action conditioning as a structured process rather than global compression. DexAC preserves dimension-level semantics via action tokenization and aligns action signals with visual dynamics through local refinement and global modulation. To address the limited high-level semantic grounding in existing world models, we further introduce a semantic branch that provides rich object-scene priors, which enables world model to capture dynamic visual details while supporting high-DoF action-conditioned video prediction. Experiments on EgoDex and EgoVerse show that combining the semantic branch with DexAC significantly improves FID, FVD, and PCK, demonstrating gains in visual-temporal realism and action-following consistency. We further verify that DexAC extends to other backbones, showing the scalability of our structured action-conditioning design. These results suggest that scaling world models to high-DoF control requires both structured action modeling and semantic grounding.

</details>

*Munachiso Samuel Nwadike, Zangir Iklassov, Ali Mekky, Zayd M. Kawakibi Zuhri, Kentaro Inui*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.AI
