TL;DR: 提出ForeAgent，一个基于MLLM的AI生成图像检测智能体框架，采用感知-判决架构聚合多视角线索，并通过事后驱动自优化策略实现持续自我进化，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法对细粒度伪造痕迹的敏感性不足，且依赖静态合成监督，灵活性和泛化性有限。需要一种能持续自我进化的检测方法。

Method: 提出ForeAgent框架：(1) Perception-Verdict架构，聚合语义、空间和频域多视角线索，由MLLM作为判决模块融合信号进行逻辑推理；(2) Hindsight-Driven Self-Refining策略，通过采样-反思-进化范式，利用真实标签作为事后信息反思失败案例并生成更高质量的推理轨迹，经双专家质量门控过滤后微调模型。

Result: 在Chameleon基准上达到82.18%准确率（比AIDE高16.41%），在AIGCDetect-Benchmark上16个生成器平均准确率93.3%，外部评估显示推理比GPT-5和GPT-5-mini更一致且因果性更强。

Conclusion: ForeAgent通过感知-判决架构和事后驱动自优化策略，有效提升了AI生成图像检测的性能和泛化能力，为深度伪造检测提供了新的智能体范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究AI生成图像检测（deepfake detection），属于多模态安全与攻防（防御方向）的典型任务。论文使用MLLM进行检测，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的安全防御子主题。现有[CANONICAL]子主题"多模态安全与攻防"完美匹配。

Importance reason: Strong technical contribution in AI-generated image detection but falls outside user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: MLLM-based detection, self-evolution via hindsight, SOTA on benchmarks, no continuous language model or video generation, negative keywords absent

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative models presents a significant challenge to existing deepfake detection methods, particularly given the widespread dissemination of highly realistic AI-generated images. Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong potential for this task, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations: insufficient sensitivity to fine-grained forensic artifacts and reliance on static synthetic supervision from frontier models, leading to limited flexibility and high-cost. To address these issues, we propose ForeAgent, an agentic forensics framework for AI-generated image detection with iterative self-evolution. First, ForeAgent adopts a Perception-Verdict architecture that aggregates multi-view cues spanning semantic, spatial, and frequency-domain features, and leverages an MLLM as a verdict module to fuse these signals for a logical-grounded verdict. Second, to enable continual self-improvement, we introduce a Hindsight-Driven Self-Refining strategy following a Sampling-Reflection-Evolution paradigm. The agent performs inference rollouts on training instances. Guided by ground-truth labels as hindsight, it reflects on failure cases and low-quality reasoning trajectories to regenerate higher-quality reasoning traces. These synthesized samples are then strictly filtered through a dual-expert quality gating module. ForeAgent continuously evolves via fine-tuning on self-curated high-quality samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ForeAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Chameleon benchmark, reaching 82.18% accuracy (+16.41% over AIDE), and achieves 93.3% mean accuracy on AIGCDetect-Benchmark across 16 generators. In addition, external evaluation shows that ForeAgent produces more consistent and causally grounded reasoning compared to GPT-5 and GPT-5-mini.

</details>


### [67] [The Inattentional Gap: Task-Conditioned Language and Vision Models Omit the Safety-Critical Signals They Can Otherwise Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26529)
*Kwan Soo Shin*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现并命名了"非注意盲视差距"（Inattentional Gap）现象：当语言或视觉模型被限定在狭窄任务上时，会抑制报告共现的安全关键信号，而这些信号在无约束条件下能被模型正常报告。该现象在放射学、驾驶文本和胸片视觉任务中普遍存在，不随模型规模增大而消失，且在推理模型中依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全评估通常衡量模型检测指定危险的能力，但实际事故往往源于未被指定的危险。现有基准测试可能高估模型在真实场景中的安全性，因为模型可能对未明确要求的危险信号视而不见。作者希望揭示这种任务条件化导致的安全信号抑制现象。

Method: 作者设计了放射学、驾驶文本和胸片视觉三类任务场景，对比模型在"有约束"（被要求完成特定任务）和"无约束"（自由报告）两种条件下对安全关键信号的报告率。测试了多种语言模型和视觉模型，包括不同规模和系列的模型，以及推理模型。

Result: 所有测试模型均表现出非注意盲视差距：任务约束条件下安全信号报告率显著低于无约束条件。该现象不随模型规模增大而减弱，在推理模型中依然存在，且不同模型系列间的差异大于同一系列不同规模间的差异。

Conclusion: 非注意盲视差距将基准测试安全性与真实世界安全性解耦：一个系统可能在评估指定的危险上得分接近完美，却对造成实际伤害的危险视而不见。这揭示了当前AI安全评估范式的根本性缺陷，需要重新思考安全评测方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究AI安全中的"非注意盲视"现象——当模型被限定在狭窄任务上时，会抑制报告共现的安全关键信号。这属于多模态安全与可靠性问题，与多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）这一[CANONICAL]子主题高度相关，因为论文直接关注安全评测的盲区与模型的安全缺陷。

Importance reason: Important safety finding but outside user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: Inattentional Gap, task-conditioned suppression, safety-critical signal omission, benchmark vs real-world safety decoupling, persists across scales and reasoning models

Abstract: AI safety is evaluated by how reliably a model detects the hazards it is told to find, yet accidents often arise from the hazard no one specified. We show that conditioning a language or vision model on a narrow task suppresses its reporting of co-present, safety-critical signals it can otherwise report, a machine analogue of human inattentional blindness arising from a different mechanism. Across radiology and driving text scenarios and chest-radiograph vision tasks, suppression appeared in every model tested, did not diminish with scale, persisted in a reasoning model, and varied more by model family than by size, while the same models reported these signals at substantially higher rates when unconstrained. We name this dissociation the Inattentional Gap and argue that it decouples measured benchmark safety from real-world safety: a system can score near-perfectly on the hazards an evaluation specifies while remaining blind to those that cause harm.

</details>


### [68] [Adversarial Diffusion Across Modalities: A Fusion Survey of Attacks, Defenses, and Evaluation for Text, Vision, and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26566)
*Abrar Alotaibi, Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文是一篇跨模态对抗扩散攻击与防御的融合综述，将文本/LLM攻击、图像分类器攻击、VLM越狱和扩散防御四条研究线整合为统一框架，提出六类扩散角色分类法和五维评估体系，并识别了当前LLM侧文献的五项弱点及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性评估在文本/LLM、图像分类器、视觉语言模型和防御四个方向各自独立发展，术语、威胁模型和基准互不连通，缺乏统一的视角来整合这些日益交叉的研究。

Method: 采用信息融合方法进行元研究综述，系统梳理50篇论文，提出六类扩散角色分类法（攻击者/防御者视角），结合威胁模型轴（攻击者知识、查询预算、目标可访问性）和五维评估框架（攻击成功率、迁移性、查询预算、困惑度、防御规避），并对比10个非扩散基线。

Result: 建立了跨模态对抗扩散的统一分类体系和评估框架，识别出当前LLM侧文献的五项系统性弱点（如缺乏标准基准、评估维度不完整等），并发布了配套目录和电子表格。

Conclusion: 对抗扩散研究需要跨模态统一视角，当前LLM侧文献存在多项不足，未来应关注标准化基准、更全面的评估维度和跨模态攻击防御的协同设计。

Classification reason: 该论文是关于文本、图像和视觉语言模型上扩散攻击与防御的综述，核心关注对抗攻击、越狱和防御，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic 'multimodal_safety_attack'。

Importance reason: This is a well-structured survey on adversarial diffusion, but it focuses on security and attack/defense taxonomies, which is outside your priority subtopics of continuous models, generation, and world models. Signals: Security-focused survey, Not aligned with priority subtopics, No continuous language/multimodal model focus, No generation or world model content, Negative keywords absent but topic irrelevant

Abstract: Adversarial evaluation of AI systems has matured along four largely disconnected tracks: diffusion-based attacks on text and large language models (LLMs), diffusion-based attacks on image classifiers, jailbreak pipelines against vision-language models, and diffusion-based input purification defenses. Each has developed its own vocabulary, threat models, and benchmarks, with denoising diffusion models emerging as a shared generative mechanism whose recipes are now actively ported between communities. This survey performs an information-fusion exercise at the meta-research level: we integrate these four tracks into a single conceptual framework with a unified taxonomy, evaluation criteria, and research agenda, focusing on the LLM-side slice. We catalog fifty published papers across four scope areas (text/LLM, image classifier, vision-language model, defense), plus four diffusion-LLM-as-victim entries and ten non-diffusion baselines against which any new attack must be compared. We propose a six-class taxonomy of diffusion roles in adversarial pipelines, augmented by a threat-model axis recording attacker knowledge, query budget, and target accessibility, and apply a five-dimension framework (attack success rate, transferability, query budget, perplexity, defense-evasion) uniformly across modalities. The review adopts a dual attacker-defender perspective: alongside the attack catalog we cover four diffusion-based defenses that form the natural evaluation backdrop for new attacks. Our critical analysis identifies five recurring weaknesses of the current LLM-side literature, and we close with a research agenda of open questions and concrete experimental designs. The companion catalog and spreadsheet are released with the paper. We are explicit that this is a narrative review with quality assessment, not a PRISMA-compliant systematic review, and discuss the implications for replication.

</details>

*Prarabdh Shukla, Ritik, Suhas Rao, Arpit Agarwal, Arjun Bhagoji*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量LLM越狱方法已被公开，但非专业恶意用户能否有效利用这些方法发起攻击尚不清楚。本文旨在验证这一担忧是否合理，并探索非专家用户能否通过自动化策略实现高效越狱攻击。

Method: 1) 提出基于多臂老虎机（MAB）的越狱攻击策略，将不同越狱方法视为"臂"，通过少量查询的噪声探索在线学习最优越狱策略，随后在利用集上应用所学策略。2) 构建FrankensteinBench基准，包含11,279条恶意查询，来自7个现有基准的手工筛选和自动增强生成，并按技术复杂度分为简单和复杂两类。

Result: 在15个SOTA开源LLM上，基于MAB的攻击平均成功率高达97%。增加查询复杂度（从简单到复杂）可使攻击成功率平均提升26%。

Conclusion: 非专业恶意用户确实能够利用自动化策略实现高效的LLM越狱攻击，这验证了安全担忧的合理性。查询复杂度的提升可进一步增强攻击效果，提示需要更强大的防御机制。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM的越狱攻击（jailbreak），提出基于多臂老虎机的攻击策略和恶意查询基准FrankensteinBench，属于多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）范畴下的LLM安全攻击方向，该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖越狱、对抗攻击等内容，匹配度较高。

Importance reason: Strong LLM security work but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: Novel bandit-based jailbreak strategy, High attack success rate (97%), Large malicious query benchmark (FrankensteinBench), Focus on non-expert attackers, Multimodal understanding & generation direction but no continuous/multimodal generation content

Abstract: With a profusion of jailbreaks for LLMs now widely known, a growing concern is that non-expert malicious actors ("the average Jane") could elicit actionable responses to malicious requests. In this work, we examine whether this concern is justified. A non-expert malicious actor requires two ingredients for a successful attack: a powerful jailbreak for their target model, acting on an effective malicious query. For the former, we propose a novel attack strategy based on the multi-armed bandit framework. This allows efficient online learning of the optimal jailbreak from a large choice set via noisy exploration on a small number of queries, with subsequent application of the learnt policy on an exploitation set. For the latter, we curate $\mathrm{FrankensteinBench}$, a safety benchmark of $11,279$ malicious queries drawn from manual curation over $7$ existing benchmarks, along with automated enhancement and generation. Each query is categorized as simple or complex by the technical expertise required to craft it. Our findings confirm the concern. Our bandit-based attack achieves success rates as high as $97\%$ on average over $15$ SoTA open-weight LLMs. Moreover, adding complexity to queries raises the attack success rate by up to $26\%$ on average across models -- making it an effective, automatable prompting strategy.

</details>

### [70] [Staying VIGILant: Mitigating Visual Laziness via Counterfactual Visual Alignment in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26387)
*Xi Xiao, Chen Liu, Chih-Ting Liao, Yunbei Zhang, Qizhen Lan, Yuxiang Wei, Lin Zhao, Janet Wang, Jianyang Gu, Muchao Ye, Tianyang Wang, Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.CV

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 高优先级 (85.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出VIGIL（Visual Information Gain In aLignment），一种基于强化学习的MLLM后训练对齐框架。通过最大化视觉输入与生成响应之间的互信息，并引入几何约束惩罚"盲目自信"（即当文本-视觉注意力被掩码时模型仍保持高置信度的情况），有效缓解了MLLM的视觉懒惰问题。实验表明，VIGIL在幻觉和推理基准上优于现有对齐方法，仅用25%的偏好数据即可达到SOTA全量数据性能，并展现出无需边界框监督的涌现式空间定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM虽然继承了LLM的强大推理能力，但容易产生与视觉输入矛盾的幻觉。机理研究表明，模型内部编码了正确的视觉证据，但在生成响应时过度依赖语言先验（即"视觉懒惰"）。现有的对齐方法（如DPO）主要基于文本优化结果级奖励，导致优化偏向语言捷径，生成的响应常与视觉证据相矛盾。因此，需要一种能够显式增强视觉-文本因果关联的对齐方法。

Method: VIGIL是一个基于强化学习的后训练框架，核心思想是最大化视觉输入与生成响应之间的互信息。具体地，引入几何约束：通过掩码文本-视觉注意力创建反事实"盲态"，惩罚模型在这种盲态下仍保持不当高置信度的"盲目自信"实例。这迫使模型在生成响应时真正依赖视觉证据，而非语言先验。VIGIL将这一约束整合到RL对齐训练中，实现从数值奖励拟合到因果视觉定位的转变。

Result: 在多个幻觉和推理基准上，VIGIL一致优于近期对齐方法。仅使用25%的偏好数据即可匹配SOTA方法的全量数据性能。此外，VIGIL展现出涌现式空间定位能力，无需显式的边界框监督即可实现空间理解。

Conclusion: VIGIL通过反事实视觉对齐有效缓解了MLLM的视觉懒惰问题，证明了显式增强视觉-文本因果关联比单纯优化文本奖励更有效。该方法在数据效率、幻觉缓解和涌现能力方面均表现出优势，为MLLM对齐提供了新的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于缓解MLLM中的视觉幻觉问题，提出VIGIL框架通过反事实视觉对齐来减少模型对语言先验的过度依赖。这直接属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses MLLM hallucination via a novel RL-based visual grounding method, highly relevant to multimodal understanding and generation. Signals: MLLM hallucination, visual laziness, RL alignment, counterfactual visual alignment, emergent spatial grounding

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend large language models (LLMs) with visual perception, enabling joint reasoning over images and text. Despite inheriting strong reasoning capabilities from LLMs, they remain prone to hallucinations that contradict their visual inputs. Mechanistic studies indicate that this weakness stems from visual laziness: MLLMs encode the correct visual evidence internally, but overly rely on strong language priors during response. Existing alignment methods, such as direct preference optimization, primarily optimize outcome-level rewards based on text. This introduces an optimization bias toward linguistic shortcuts, leading to responses that often contradict the visual evidence. To address this, we propose Visual Information Gain In aLignment (VIGIL), a reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training framework that shifts the focus from numerical reward fitting to causal visual grounding. VIGIL introduces a geometric constraint that explicitly maximizes the mutual information between the visual input and the generated response. We achieve this by penalizing "blind confidence" instances where the model remains improperly certain even when textual-visual attention is masked to create a counterfactual blind state. Extensive experiments show that VIGIL consistently outperforms recent alignment methods across hallucination and reasoning benchmarks without compromising text-only capabilities. Our approach matches the full-data performance of state-of-the-art methods using only 25% of the preference data and even demonstrates emergent spatial grounding capabilities without explicit bounding box supervision.

</details>

Classification reason: 论文核心贡献是缓解多模态大模型在医学报告生成中的幻觉问题（hallucination），属于多模态幻觉与可靠性方向。该论文提出的 Risk-Conditioned Causal Grounding Attention 旨在增强生成文本的解剖学基础，减少诊断幻觉，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic '多模态幻觉与可靠性'。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical report generation and hallucination reduction, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions. Signals: Medical domain, hallucination reduction, causal grounding, TAVR planning, multimodal understanding generation

Abstract: Transcatheter Aortic Valve Replacement (TAVR) planning requires meticulous multimodal reasoning. However, adapting Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to this high-stakes domain is severely impeded by diagnostic hallucinations, where generated text lacks anatomical grounding. To address this, TAVR-VLM is introduced: a novel framework featuring Risk-Conditioned Causal Grounding Attention (R-CGA) that instantiates a model-internal ``Risk $\rightarrow$ Region $\rightarrow$ Word'' structural grounding pathway. R-CGA compresses multimodal inputs into a causal risk bottleneck, purifying dense visual features into a global risk mask. During autoregressive generation, a support-projected causal consistency objective constrains token-level grounding within the risk-defined support mask. Evaluated on $\text{M}^3\text{TAVR}$, a comprehensive 1,482-patient cohort, TAVR-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art. It achieves an AUROC of 0.896, boosts CIDEr to 0.936, and drastically reduces the hallucination rate to 8.1\%, thereby improving interpretability for evidence-based surgical AI.

</details>

Classification reason: 该论文研究医学VQA中MLLM的置信度校准问题，旨在提升模型输出的可靠性和忠实性，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）这一[CANONICAL]子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VQA calibration, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword domain. Signals: Medical VQA, calibration, multimodal LLM, Brier loss, contrastive alignment

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) applied to Medical Visual Question Answering (VQA) tend to produce overconfident outputs regardless of actual correctness, and existing verbalized confidence calibration methods, developed primarily for text only LLMs, do not account for the multimodal nature of medical image understanding. This work proposes a training based framework that finetunes MLLMs to improve their calibration using a composite loss function combining a Brier style calibration term, an anchor regularizer that prevents confidence collapse toward extreme values, a contrastive image text alignment term, and a KL based model stabilization term. The alignment signal is derived from a $2 \times 2$ factorial perturbation design that crosses image presence with text integrity, probing the reliance of the model on visual modality input versus language priors. Finally, a top K KL divergence regularizer is used to protect the answering ability of the model during finetuning. Across three Medical VQA benchmarks and two architectures (MedGemma 4B IT and Qwen2 VL 7B Instruct), our method reduces calibration error by 60% or more, and improves discrimination by 26% or more, while preserving predictive accuracy. On average across benchmarks, the technique outperforms prompting based, sampling based, and training based approaches, and ablation experiments confirm that each component of the loss function is indeed necessary for improving the calibration. All code for the experiments is publicly available.

</details>

### [73] [ReasonCLIP-58M: Visually Grounded Commonsense Reasoning Supervision for CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26794)

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReasonCLIP-58M，一个通过大规模推理监督信号对 CLIP 进行持续预训练的框架。构建了 ReasonLite-42M（开放形式推理描述）和 ReasonPro-16M（类别结构化推理监督）两个数据集及 RCLIP-Bench 评测基准。训练得到的 ReasonCLIP 在保持描述性对齐的同时，显著提升了视觉常识推理和组合推理能力，并增强了零样本检索性能。作为 LLaVA-NeXT 等 MLLM 的即插即用视觉编码器，无需额外推理成本即可带来一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 及其变体被广泛用作多模态系统的视觉骨干，但其预训练仍以描述性图文对齐为主，缺乏对视觉常识推理和组合推理能力的显式支持。下游应用日益需要这些推理能力，而现有 CLIP 模型在不改变架构的情况下能否支持此类推理尚不明确。

Method: 提出两阶段持续预训练框架：第一阶段将大规模推理信号整合到 CLIP 中，同时保持描述性对齐；第二阶段引入类别结构化的推理监督。构建了两个互补数据集：ReasonLite-42M（开放形式、视觉可验证的推理描述）和 ReasonPro-16M（类别特定推理监督），以及 RCLIP-Bench 诊断评测基准。

Result: ReasonCLIP 在视觉常识推理和组合推理任务上显著优于原始 CLIP，同时零样本检索性能也得到提升。作为 LLaVA-NeXT 等 MLLM 的视觉编码器，无需额外推理成本即可带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结构化推理监督能够增强 CLIP 风格视觉表征的表达能力，在不改变架构的前提下使 CLIP 支持视觉常识推理和组合推理，且作为即插即用组件可提升下游 MLLM 的性能。

Classification reason: 该论文通过大规模推理监督信号对CLIP进行持续预训练，以增强其视觉常识推理和组合推理能力，属于多模态视觉语言模型的对齐与表征增强。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）中的"多模态对齐"方向高度吻合，因为论文核心是改进视觉-语言表征的对齐质量，使其具备更强的推理能力。

Importance reason: Focuses on CLIP reasoning supervision, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: CLIP continual pretraining, reasoning supervision, visual commonsense, compositional reasoning, MLLM encoder improvement

Abstract: CLIP and its variants are widely adopted visual backbones in multimodal systems, but their pretraining remains dominated by descriptive image-text alignment. As downstream applications increasingly demand visually grounded commonsense inference and compositional reasoning, it remains unclear whether CLIP-style encoders can support such reasoning without architectural changes. To address this, we present ReasonCLIP-58M, a continual pretraining framework that integrates large-scale reasoning supervision into CLIP-style models through our two-stage strategy, which progressively integrates reasoning signals while preserving descriptive alignment, followed by category-structured reasoning supervision. To support this framework, we construct two complementary datasets and a benchmark: ReasonLite-42M, with open-form, visually verifiable reasoning captions; ReasonPro-16M, with category-specific reasoning supervision; and RCLIP-Bench for diagnostic evaluation of visually grounded reasoning. We train a family of ReasonCLIP that improves visually grounded commonsense and compositional reasoning while also enhancing zero-shot retrieval performance. As a drop-in visual encoder for multimodal large language models such as LLaVA-NeXT, ReasonCLIP delivers consistent gains without additional inference cost, demonstrating that structured reasoning supervision enhances the expressive capacity of CLIP-style visual representations. All datasets, models, and training code are available at .

</details>


### [74] [The Red Queen Gödel Machine: Co-Evolving Agents and Their Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26294)
*Alex Iacob, Andrej Jovanović, William F. Shen, Daniel Burkhardt, Meghdad Kurmanji, Nurbek Tastan, Lorenzo Sani, Niccolò Alberto Elia Venanzi, Ambroise Odonnat, Zeyu Cao, Bill Marino, Xinchi Qiu, Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.NE, cs.AI, cs.MA

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Red Queen Gödel Machine (RQGM)，一种在非平稳效用函数下进行递归自我改进的演化框架。通过将评估器也纳入演化循环，RQGM 实现了 agent 与 evaluator 的协同演化，在可验证编码任务、科学论文写作与评审、奥林匹克级证明写作与评分等场景中均优于现有自我改进方法，并能够纠正评审者对 AI 生成论文的过度接受偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进智能体假设评估标准是固定的（如固定的验证器、基准或标注数据集），忽略了演化中物种适应环境、环境也随之变化的核心特征。本文旨在将这一原则引入递归自我改进，使评估成为改进循环的一部分，从而超越静态基准的限制。

Method: 提出 Red Queen Gödel Machine (RQGM)，一种演化框架，通过受控的效用演化实现非平稳效用下的递归自我改进。搜索被组织为多个 epoch，每个 epoch 内评估标准固定，而效用函数可在 epoch 边界更新，从而保证每个 epoch 内的自我改进保证成立。在编码任务中，引入 agent-as-a-judge 代码审查信号作为互补信号；在科学论文写作与评审中，实现作者与评审者的协同演化，并引入对抗性目标来纠正评审偏差。

Result: 在可验证编码任务上，RQGM 比先前 SOTA 提高了测试通过率，且使用 1.35x-1.72x 更少的 token。在科学论文写作中，协同演化的作者在多样化 agent-as-a-judge 评审面板下达到 1.78x-1.86x 更高的接受率；协同演化的评分者达到 9% 更高的真实准确率。在论文评审中，最强基线评审者对 AI 生成论文的接受率高达人类水平的 1.91 倍，RQGM 通过对抗性目标纠正了这一偏差。

Conclusion: RQGM 框架成功地将演化原则引入递归自我改进，通过使评估器也成为演化的一部分，在多个领域超越了静态基准的自我改进方法。该框架能够自动发现并纠正评估偏差，为构建更鲁棒、更自适应的自我改进系统提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Red Queen Gödel Machine (RQGM)，一种在非平稳效用函数下进行递归自我改进的演化框架，核心涉及 agent 与 evaluator 的协同演化、对抗性目标以及偏好对齐。这与多模态强化学习与对齐方向（multimodal_rl_alignment）中的偏好学习、对齐、奖励建模等主题高度相关，且论文在代码审查、科学论文评审等场景中使用了 agent-as-a-judge 的对齐信号。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on multi-agent co-evolution and evaluation, which is not aligned with the user's priority on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: Recursive self-improvement, Co-evolving agents and evaluators, Non-stationary utility, Agent-as-a-judge, Scientific paper writing and review

Abstract: Self-improving agents are state-of-the-art (SOTA) on agentic coding benchmarks and have recently been extended to general domains. However, their search methods generally assume a stationary evaluation criterion: a fixed verifier, benchmark, or labeled dataset that remains valid as the agent improves. This ignores a central feature of evolution: species adapt as their environments change with them. We aim to bring the same principle to recursive self-improvement, making evaluation part of the improvement loop and opening search to evolving evaluators, adversarial objectives, and dynamic utilities that may surpass static benchmarks. We introduce the Red Queen Godel Machine (RQGM), an evolutionary framework for recursive self-improvement under non-stationary utilities. The RQGM makes this possible through controlled utility evolution: search is organized into epochs with a fixed within-epoch evaluation criterion, while the utility can be updated at epoch boundaries, so self-improvement guarantees hold per epoch as the objective evolves across them. We begin by showing that even on verifiable coding tasks, the RQGM improves test pass rate over the prior SOTA by adding a complementary agent-as-a-judge code-review signal. This signal is cheaper and the RQGM uses 1.35x-1.72x fewer tokens. We then turn to scientific paper writing and reviewing, and Olympiad-level proof writing and grading, where the RQGM improves performance over prior self-improving agents: co-evolved writers reach 1.78x-1.86x higher acceptance rates under a diverse agent-as-a-judge panel, while co-evolved graders reach 9% higher ground-truth accuracy. In paper reviewing, the strongest baseline reviewer over-accepts AI-generated papers at up to 1.91x the human rate. The RQGM corrects this by introducing an adversarial objective that discovers reviewers equally stringent on AI and human work.

</details>


### [75] [Paying More Attention to Visual Tokens in Self-Evolving Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27373)
*Shravan Venkatraman, Ritesh Thawkar, Omkar Thawakar, Rao Muhammad Anwer, Hisham Cholakkal, Salman Khan, Fahad Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISE（Visual Invariance Self-Evolution），一种纯无监督的自演化框架，通过几何不变性奖励和语义不变性奖励直接正则化多模态大模型的视觉条件化策略，解决现有自演化LMM中解码器依赖语言先验而非视觉内容的"视觉欠条件化"问题。在18个基准上验证，使用Qwen3-VL-2B在COCO上提升+16.85 CIDEr，TextCaps上提升+19.66 CIDEr，物体幻觉降低5.0 Chair-I点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化多模态大模型（LMM）使用多角色自博弈和自一致性奖励机制，只优化答案一致性而不确保解码器关注视觉内容，导致模型依赖统计语言先验而非图像信息进行生成，即"视觉欠条件化"问题。这严重影响了图像描述和视觉问答等视觉语言理解任务的性能。

Method: 提出VISE框架，包含两个互补的基于不变性的奖励：1）几何不变性奖励（Geometric Invariance Reward），强制模型在已知几何变换（如裁剪、旋转）下保持空间一致性；2）语义不变性奖励（Semantic Invariance Reward），通过扰动预测区域使模型识别证据缺失，惩罚不依赖视觉证据的生成。整个框架在单一模型内运行，无需专家角色、外部奖励模型或标注，仅使用原始无标签图像训练。

Result: 在18个基准上评估，以Qwen3-VL-2B为基础模型，COCO上+16.85 CIDEr，TextCaps上+19.66 CIDEr，物体幻觉降低5.0 Chair-I点。方法在四个模型家族和不同规模上均表现出泛化能力。

Conclusion: VISE通过直接正则化视觉条件化策略，有效解决了自演化LMM中的视觉欠条件化问题，在纯无监督设置下显著提升了视觉语言理解性能，且具有良好的跨模型泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出VISE框架，通过几何不变性奖励和语义不变性奖励来正则化多模态模型的视觉条件化策略，属于无监督自演化方法，核心是对多模态模型进行偏好优化和对齐，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Novel unsupervised self-evolution method for visual token attention in LMMs, but subtopic is not a priority. Signals: visual under-conditioning, self-evolving LMM, geometric invariance reward, semantic invariance reward, Qwen3-VL-2B

Abstract: Recently, self-evolving large multimodal models (LMMs) have received attention for improving visual reasoning in a purely unsupervised setting. However, multi-role self-play and self-consistency reward schemes in existing self-evolving LMMs optimize answer agreement without ensuring the decoder attends to visual content, relying instead on statistical language priors to produce self consistent outputs. This leads to a persistent failure mode we term visual under-conditioning, where the decoder relies on language priors rather than the image during generation, manifesting as insufficient attention to visual tokens. As a result, current self-evolving LMMs struggle on vision--language understanding tasks such as image captioning and visual question answering. To address this, we propose VISE (Visual Invariance Self-Evolution), a purely unsupervised self-evolving framework that directly regularizes the model's visual conditioning policy through two complementary invariance-based rewards: a geometric invariance reward that enforces spatial consistency under known transformations, and a semantic invariance reward that penalizes evidence-agnostic generation by requiring the model to recognize the absence of evidence when predicted regions are perturbed. VISE operates within a single model without specialist roles, external reward models, or annotations, and is trained on raw unlabeled images. Experiments on 18 benchmarks demonstrate the efficacy of our approach. Using Qwen3-VL-2B as the base model, VISE achieves gains of $+16.85$ CIDEr on COCO and $+19.66$ CIDEr on TextCaps, reduces object hallucination by $5.0$ Chair-I points, and generalizes across four model families and scales. Our code and models are available at

</details>


### [76] [GEOALIGN: Geometric Rollout Curation for Robust LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26917)
*Ting Zhou, Zhenqing Ling, Yiyang Zhao, Ying Shen, Daoyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GEOALIGN提出了一种轻量级插件，通过检测和修正在线RL中方向不一致的rollout（生成样本），来稳定LLM对齐训练。该方法利用隐空间中奖励排序的位移方向构建共识原型，剔除偏离的rollout，在对话对齐和数学推理任务上提升了性能并减少了训练震荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习用于LLM对齐时，在噪声或错误指定的奖励信号下训练不稳定。作者发现一个称为"方向不一致性"的失败模式：批次中少数高奖励rollout产生的表征空间偏好方向与批次多数严重不一致，导致高方差和不稳定的更新。

Method: GEOALIGN是一个轻量级插件，用于迭代策略优化中的rollout筛选。它：(i) 构建同prompt内的偏好对；(ii) 学习一个在线投影器，对每个rollout的隐状态进行投影，集中奖励排序后的位移方向；(iii) 通过rollout方向与批次共识原型的角度偏差检测方向不一致的rollout，并用同prompt内的稳定替代方案进行修正。该方法仅需前向传播，开销极小。

Result: 在对话对齐（使用学习到的奖励模型）和数学推理（使用二元验证奖励）两个场景中，GEOALIGN相比PF-PPO、PAR、PODS和Seed-GRPO等方法，提升了最终性能并减少了训练震荡。

Conclusion: 隐空间中的方向一致性可以作为在线LLM RL的有效可靠性信号，GEOALIGN通过简单的rollout筛选机制即可显著提升训练稳定性与最终性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究在线强化学习用于LLM对齐，属于多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）范畴。虽然论文主要聚焦文本LLM的对齐，但其方法（rollout curation、reward信号下的策略优化）与多模态RL对齐方向高度相关，且该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖RLHF、偏好学习等核心内容。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL training stability for LLM alignment, not directly on priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: LLM alignment, reinforcement learning, rollout curation, directional inconsistency, training stability

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning is widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with reward signals, yet training can be unstable under noisy or misspecified rewards. We identify a failure mode we call directional inconsistency: within a batch, a small set of high-reward rollouts induces representation-space preference directions that sharply disagree with the batch majority, resulting in high-variance and destabilizing updates. We propose geoalign, a lightweight plug-in for rollout curation in iterative policy optimization. Geoalign (i) forms within-prompt preference pairs, (ii) learns an online projector on per-rollout hidden states to concentrate reward-ordered displacement directions, and (iii) detects directionally inconsistent rollouts via their angular deviation from a batch consensus prototype and rectifies them with within-prompt stable alternatives. Geoalign is forward-pass only and adds negligible overhead. Across dialogue alignment with a learned reward model and mathematical reasoning with binary verified rewards, Geoalign improves final performance and reduces training oscillation, outperforming PF-PPO, PAR, PODS, and Seed-GRPO. These results suggest latent directional consensus as an effective reliability signal for online LLM RL.

</details>

### [77] [Position Rebinding Cache Reuse: Replay-Free Visual Revisiting for Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26631)
*Mengzhao Wang, Yanli Ji, Wangmeng Zuo, Peng Ye, Chongjun Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (68.88) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出位置重绑定缓存复用（PRCR），通过存储原始视觉KV缓存及其空间坐标，在解码时重新分配位置兼容的坐标并重绑定键，实现无需重放token的视觉证据复用，避免历史缓存位置绑定导致的注意力失真和解码崩溃，在多个多模态推理基准上平均提升5%准确率并大幅降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交错多模态推理方法在生成过程中需要反复重放视觉token来重新访问视觉证据，计算开销巨大。直接复用历史视觉KV缓存虽然看似捷径，但会导致严重的自回归解码崩溃，因为缓存的视觉键已绑定到原始位置上下文，与当前解码上下文不兼容。因此需要一种无需重放、能正确重建视觉证据的缓存复用机制。

Method: 提出位置重绑定缓存复用（PRCR），核心思想是：存储原始视觉KV缓存及其空间坐标，在需要复用视觉证据时，根据当前解码状态重新分配位置兼容的坐标，对选中的缓存条目进行键重绑定，然后将重建的缓存注入到当前解码器的活跃缓存中。这样既复用了历史视觉证据，又保持了文本位置连续性和相对视觉结构。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上，PRCR达到或超过token重放方法的性能，平均准确率提升5%，视觉重访计算量减少数万倍。

Conclusion: PRCR证明了通过位置重绑定可以有效解决历史KV缓存的位置绑定问题，实现高效、无需重放的视觉证据复用，为交错多模态推理提供了一种计算高效的缓存级解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究交错多模态推理中的视觉证据重用问题，属于多模态推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 (multimodal_reasoning_cot) 高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly addresses multimodal reasoning efficiency with a novel cache reuse method, aligning with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: multimodal reasoning, KV cache reuse, position rebinding, autoregressive decoding, visual revisiting

Abstract: Interleaved multimodal reasoning improves visual grounding by revisiting visual evidence during multi-step generation, yet existing methods typically rely on token replay, repeatedly forwarding selected visual tokens. A natural shortcut is to reuse the historical visual key-value (KV) cache directly. However, we identify a critical failure mode of this strategy: cached visual keys are already bound to their original positional context. Such stale positional binding distorts attention under later decoding contexts and can trigger severe autoregressive decoding collapse. This failure suggests that effective cache reuse requires reconstructing visual evidence under positions compatible with the current decoding state, rather than directly copying position-bound historical cache entries. To this end, we propose Position Rebinding Cache Reuse (PRCR), a cache-level framework for replay-free visual revisiting. PRCR stores raw visual KV cache together with their original spatial coordinates, then reassigns position-compatible coordinates to select entries and rebinds their keys before injecting the reconstructed cache into the active decoder cache. This design reuses historical visual evidence while preserving textual positional continuity and relative visual structure. Experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that PRCR achieves replay-level or better performance, improving average accuracy by 5 percent and reducing visual-revisiting computation by up to tens of thousands of times.

</details>


### [78] [Bridging Vision and Language Concepts through Optimal Transport Semantic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26891)
*Chenyang Zhang, Anqi Dong, Guangming Zhu, Nuoye Xiong, Siyuan Wang, Lin Mei, Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出最优传输流概念瓶颈模型（OTF-CBM），将概念对齐重新建模为动态跨模态传输过程，通过逆最优传输学习语义代价、非平衡最优传输流匹配建模视觉块与文本概念间的语义转换，以及基于速度的概念激活实现可解释的跨模态推理，在分类准确率和概念忠实性上取得更优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言概念瓶颈模型（CBM）通常依赖预对齐编码器或全局余弦相似度，这掩盖了细粒度概念定位，无法反映真实的语义几何结构。作者希望将概念对齐从静态投影重新思考为动态跨模态传输过程，以更好地捕捉视觉与文本概念之间的语义对应关系。

Method: OTF-CBM 包含三个关键组件：(1) 通过逆最优传输（Inverse Optimal Transport）学习数据驱动的语义代价函数，衡量跨模态距离；(2) 基于非平衡最优传输的流匹配（Unbalanced Optimal Transport Flow Matching）建模视觉块与文本概念之间的语义转换；(3) 基于速度的概念激活（Velocity-based Concept Activation），无需 ODE 积分即可捕获可解释的几何关系。

Result: 实验表明，OTF-CBM 在分类准确率和概念忠实性（concept faithfulness）上均优于现有方法，为可解释的跨模态推理提供了新的几何与动力学视角。

Conclusion: 本文通过最优传输流将概念对齐重新定义为动态传输过程，验证了该几何与动力学视角在可解释跨模态推理中的有效性，为未来概念瓶颈模型的设计提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于最优传输流的概念瓶颈模型（OTF-CBM），通过动态跨模态传输对齐视觉与文本概念，属于可解释的跨模态推理方法。映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot），因为其核心是视觉-语言概念对齐与可解释推理。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel optimal transport flow for concept alignment, directly relevant to continuous multimodal representation and interpretable reasoning. Signals: continuous multimodal representation, optimal transport flow matching, concept bottleneck model, interpretable cross-modal reasoning, semantic cost learning

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) promise transparent reasoning by predicting through human-interpretable concepts, yet their effectiveness fundamentally depends on how well visual and textual representations are aligned or matched. Existing vision-language CBMs often rely on pre-aligned encoders or global cosine similarity, which obscures fine-grained concept localization and fails to reflect true semantic geometry. In this work, we rethink concept alignment as a dynamic cross-modal transport process instead of static projection and propose the Optimal Transport Flow Concept Bottleneck Model (OTF-CBM). It first learns a data-driven semantic cost via Inverse Optimal Transport to measure cross-modal distances, and then performs unbalanced optimal-transport-based flow matching to model semantic transitions between visual patches and textual concepts. With velocity-based concept activation, OTF-CBM captures interpretable geometric relations without ODE integration. Experiments further show that OTF-CBM achieves superior classification accuracy and concept faithfulness, offering a new geometric and dynamical perspective for interpretable cross-modal reasoning.

</details>

### [79] [See & Sniff: Learning Visuo-Olfactory Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27307)
*Seongyu Kim, Seungwoo Lee, Hyeonggon Ryu, Joon Son Chung, Arda Senocak*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

TL;DR: 本文提出 See & Sniff，一个自监督的视觉-嗅觉联合表征学习框架。通过构建 SmellNet-V 数据集（利用气味在语义类别内视觉不变性，将纯嗅觉数据与网络图像配对），并采用密集局部对齐方法学习跨模态表征，同时自然产生气味显著性图用于气味源空间定位。该方法在气味分类、跨模态检索和气味定位任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型主要关注视觉与语言、音频、触觉的融合，嗅觉（olfaction）由于缺乏配对的视觉-嗅觉数据而几乎未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立视觉与嗅觉之间的跨模态学习范式。

Method: 1) 构建 SmellNet-V 数据集：利用"气味身份在语义类别内对视觉变换具有不变性"这一洞察，将纯嗅觉样本与语义对齐的网络图像进行合成配对，无需昂贵的联合采集。2) 提出 See & Sniff 框架：采用自监督学习，通过密集局部对齐（dense local alignment）学习视觉与嗅觉的联合表征，并自然产生气味显著性图（smell saliency maps）用于气味源的空间定位。3) 引入像素级气味定位任务及对应评测基准。

Result: 该方法在仅使用气味模态的分类任务上比纯嗅觉基线提升 7%；在跨模态检索和气味定位任务上也展现出良好的泛化能力，建立了视觉-嗅觉多模态感知的新方向。

Conclusion: 本文证明了视觉-嗅觉跨模态学习的可行性，通过合成配对数据和自监督对齐方法有效克服了数据稀缺问题，为多模态感知开辟了新的研究方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉与嗅觉的跨模态联合表征学习，核心任务包括跨模态检索（visuo-olfactory retrieval）和气味定位，属于多模态检索范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）子主题涵盖跨模态检索，与此论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel visuo-olfactory direction but far from user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Novel cross-modal pairing method, SmellNet-V dataset, smell saliency maps, no direct overlap with priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: While modern multimodal models integrate vision with language, audio, or touch, olfaction remains largely unexplored due to the lack of paired visuo-olfactory data. We introduce SmellNet-V, a scalable visuo-olfactory dataset built on the insight that odor identity is largely invariant to visual transformations within a semantic category. This allows us to synthetically pair smell-only samples with semantically aligned in-the-wild web images, converting a unimodal olfactory dataset into a cross-modal benchmark without costly co-collection. Building on this dataset, we propose See & Sniff, a self-supervised framework that learns joint visuo-olfactory representations via dense local alignment and naturally produces smell saliency maps for spatial grounding of odor sources. We further introduce pixel-level smell localization task and a benchmark for evaluation. Our method surpasses smell-only baselines by 7% in smell classification from smell alone and generalizes to cross-modal retrieval and smell localization, establishing visuo-olfactory learning as a new direction in multimodal perception.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_benchmark'></div>

### [80] [DocArena: Turning Raw Documents into Controllable Training Environments for Document Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26122)
*Jiamian Wang, Ruiyi Zhang, Tong Yu, Jing Shi, Samyadeep Basu, Rajiv Jain, Zhiqiang Tao, Tong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DocArena提出了一种全自动的数据构建流程，利用MLLM将原始文档转化为可控的多模态文档搜索训练环境，无需人工标注。构建了涵盖16个领域、49种语言、8336份文档的DocArena-79K数据集，并设计了将视觉感知与策略模型解耦的Doc-Search Agent框架。实验表明，基于DocArena数据训练的智能体在检索准确率和问答质量上均达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体训练环境主要基于文本，难以处理多模态数据，且缺乏可控性和可扩展性。同时，构建高质量训练环境通常需要大量人工标注，成本高昂。因此，需要一种自动化、可扩展且支持多模态文档的训练环境构建方法。

Method: DocArena包含三个步骤：(1) 通过MLLM视觉感知对原始文档进行结构化索引；(2) 分析跨页面信息分布，构建推理密集型QA对；(3) 通过MLLM进行级联质量保证。同时设计了Doc-Search Agent，将视觉感知与策略模型解耦，使基于文本的LLM能够作为多模态文档检索和QA的推理骨干。

Result: 在6个多模态文档场景和7个基于文本的QA基准上，基于DocArena数据训练的智能体在检索准确率和问答质量上均取得最佳性能。进一步的行为分析验证了所构建训练环境的有效性和可控性。

Conclusion: DocArena提供了一种无需人工标注、可扩展的多模态文档搜索训练环境构建方法，能够有效提升搜索智能体的检索和问答能力，为多模态文档搜索领域提供了新的数据构建范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了DocArena，一个将原始文档转化为可控训练环境的自动化数据构建流程，并构建了DocArena-79K多模态文档搜索与问答数据集，同时设计了Doc-Search Agent框架进行评测。核心贡献在于构建多模态文档搜索的训练环境与评测基准，因此映射到[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Strong multimodal document search benchmark but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal document search, MLLM-based data curation, Agent training environment, Decoupled visual perception, 16 domains 49 languages

Abstract: Recent methods train search agents via reinforcement learning from (question, answer, evidence) tuples without requiring expert trajectories. The tuples serve as the training environment, and whose properties directly shape what search strategies and generalization abilities the agent can develop. While prior works have made encouraging progress in improving training data quality, existing environments remain predominantly text-based and existing approaches can struggle to construct training environments that are controllable, scalable, and account for multimodal data. Given this, we propose DocArena, a fully automated data curation pipeline building on the practical need for multimodal document search and question-answering. It transforms raw document collections into training environments for search agents without any human annotation. The pipeline first structures and indexes documents through MLLM-based visual perception, then profiles and leverage the cross-page information distribution to construct reasoning-intensive QA pairs, as well as performs cascaded quality assurance operations via MLLM. We introduce DocArena-79K with QA pairs from 8,336 documents spanning 16 domains and 49 languages. We further design a Doc-Search agent infrastructure that decouples visual perception from the policy model, allowing text-based LLMs to serve as the reasoning backbone for multimodal document retrieval and QA. Under a unified evaluation framework where only the policy model differs, experiments on six multimodal document scenarios and seven text-based QA benchmarks show that agents trained on DocArena data achieve the best performance on both retrieval accuracy and QA quality. Further analysis on agent search behaviors confirms the effectiveness and controllability of the constructed training environment.

</details>


### [81] [From Hallucination to Grounding: Diagnosing Visual Spatial Intelligence via CRISP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26535)
*Zhixing Li, Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (63.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CRISP，一种基于结构诊断的多模态评测范式，通过度量3D场景图和oracle干预协议，解耦视觉空间推理中的感知与推理能力，揭示闭源模型存在感知-推理脱节而开源模型缺乏多跳组合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评测常混淆语言先验与真实空间推理能力，无法区分模型是"猜对"还是真正"感知并推理"。需要一种能解耦感知瓶颈与推理能力的诊断性评测方法。

Method: 提出CRISP评测范式，利用度量3D场景图（metric 3D Scene Graphs）和oracle干预协议，通过一致性（consistency）——隐式感知与显式推理之间的对齐程度——来评估视觉空间智能，从而将潜在推理能力与感知瓶颈解耦。

Result: 揭示了闭源模型拥有强大的隐式推理引擎，但存在度量估计不准确以及未能利用其隐式结构表示的关键缺陷；开源模型则根本上受限于缺乏多跳组合推理能力。

Conclusion: CRISP提供了一种超越端到端后训练的严格多模态对齐路线图，将评测焦点从"通过语言先验猜对"转向"真正感知、验证和推理"。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CRISP，一种新的视觉空间智能评测范式，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Provides a diagnostic benchmark for spatial reasoning in VLMs, but its focus on evaluation rather than model building limits direct applicability to your priority subtopics. Signals: VLM evaluation, spatial reasoning, perception-reasoning disconnect, oracle intervention, 3D scene graphs

Abstract: Current VLM evaluations often conflate language priors with genuine spatial reasoning. To address this, we introduce CRISP, a novel structural-diagnostic evaluation paradigm that assesses visual spatial intelligence through consistency, the alignment between implicit perception and explicit reasoning. Unlike traditional black-box QA, CRISP utilizes metric 3D Scene Graphs and an oracle intervention protocol to decouple latent reasoning capabilities from perceptual bottlenecks. This granular diagnosis uncovers a systematic perception-reasoning disconnect. Crucially, we reveal that while proprietary models possess robust latent reasoning engines, they suffer from inaccurate metric estimation and a critical failure to leverage their implicit structural representations. Conversely, open-source models remain fundamentally bottlenecked by their lack of multi-hop compositional reasoning. By shifting the focus from merely ``guessing correctly'' via language priors to genuinely ``perceiving, verifying, and reasoning,'' CRISP offers a rigorous roadmap for multimodal alignment beyond end-to-end post-training. The code and dataset are available at .

</details>


### [82] [DiCoBench: Benchmarking Multi-Image Fine-Grained Perception via Differential and Commonality Visual Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26602)
*Geng Li, Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiCoBench，一个面向多图像细粒度感知的高分辨率评测基准，包含差异视觉线索和共性视觉线索两个渐进轨道共765个样本，评估了18个MLLM，发现模型与人类准确率（98.3%）之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评测基准多依赖显式文本线索或低分辨率输入，无法评估模型自主感知高分辨率图像中隐式视觉线索的能力，因此需要一个新的高分辨率多图像细粒度感知基准。

Method: 构建了包含765个精心筛选样本的DiCoBench基准，分为差异视觉线索和共性视觉线索两个渐进轨道，涵盖8种感知任务。采用多项选择题形式，使用接近2K分辨率的高清图像，消除了评估指标偏差。

Result: 对18个MLLM的广泛评估显示，人类准确率为98.3%，而最佳模型在微观细节捕捉方面表现显著不足，揭示了当前MLLM在高分辨率多图像细粒度感知上的巨大差距。

Conclusion: DiCoBench可作为具有挑战性的测试平台，推动未来在高分辨率自主多图像感知方向的研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多图像细粒度感知评测基准 DiCoBench，属于多模态评测与基准方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Benchmark for multi-image fine-grained perception is useful but not aligned with user's priority on continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: MLLM benchmark, high-resolution multi-image, fine-grained perception, human gap, not generation or world model

Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive fine-grained perception capabilities. However, existing benchmarks predominantly rely on explicit textual cues or low-resolution inputs, failing to evaluate a model's ability to autonomously perceive implicit visual cues in high-resolution. To bridge this gap, we introduce DiCoBench, a comprehensive, multi-image high-resolution benchmark designed for cross-image fine-grained perception. DiCoBench consists of 765 meticulously curated samples categorized into two progressive tracks: Differential Visual Cues and Commonality Visual Cues, covering 8 distinct perception tasks. By formulating the benchmark as a multiple-choice question task and utilizing high-resolution imagery (approaching 2K), we eliminate evaluation metric bias and pose a substantial challenge to current state-of-the-art MLLMs. Our extensive evaluation of 18 diverse MLLMs reveals a striking performance gap compared to human accuracy (98.3\%), with top-performing models struggling significantly with micro-scale detail capture. We believe DiCoBench will serve as a challenging testbed to drive future research in autonomous, high-resolution multi-image perception.

</details>


### [83] [What We are Missing in Multimodal LLM Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26348)
*Po-han Li, Shenghui Chen, Sandeep Chinchali, Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 高优先级 (83.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了当前多模态大语言模型（MLLM）评测基准的局限性，指出现有评测大多局限于孤立任务，无法有效衡量模型跨模态信息整合能力。作者识别了四个关键评测缺口：时空连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性和选择性注意，并强调填补这些缺口对衡量多模态智能的真实进展至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM的能力快速进步，但评测方法未能同步发展。现有评测基准局限于孤立任务，难以揭示模型是否真正整合了跨模态信息，亟需系统性地审视评测体系中的缺失。

Method: 论文通过文献综述和基准分类学分析，系统梳理了现有MLLM评测基准的类别，识别出四个关键评测缺口（时空连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性、选择性注意），并对每个缺口进行了深入讨论。

Result: 识别出现有MLLM评测在四个关键维度上的缺失：1) 时间-空间连贯性评测不足；2) 物理世界理解能力缺乏有效评测；3) 跨模态一致性缺乏系统评估；4) 选择性注意能力未被充分考察。

Conclusion: 填补这些评测缺口对于准确衡量多模态智能的真实进展、揭示模型能力边界至关重要，为未来MLLM评测基准的设计提供了方向性指导。

Classification reason: 该论文系统性地审视了现有MLLM评测基准的不足，识别了时间-空间连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性和选择性注意等评测缺口，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Identifies critical gaps in MLLM evaluation that directly impact understanding of multimodal integration, a core user priority. Signals: MLLM evaluation gaps, multimodal consistency, physical world understanding, temporal-spatial coherence, selective attention

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can process diverse inputs, e.g., text, images, audio, and video, and generate textual responses. While their capabilities have advanced rapidly, evaluation of such models has not kept pace. Most existing evaluation benchmarks are limited to isolated tasks and reveal little about whether a model integrates information across modalities. We examine current means for evaluating MLLMs and review the existing benchmark taxonomy to identify gaps, including temporal-spatial coherence, physical world understanding, multimodal consistency, and selective attention. Addressing these gaps is essential for measuring real progress in multimodal intelligence and exposing capability boundaries.

</details>


### [84] [MKG-RAG-Bench: Benchmarking Retrieval in Multimodal Knowledge Graph-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26458)
*Xiaochen Wang, Bao Hoang, Han Liu, Ting Wang, Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MKG-RAG-Bench，一个专门用于评估多模态知识图谱检索增强生成（MKG-RAG）中检索环节的跨领域基准，包含通用和医学两个领域的多模态知识图谱及对齐的问答数据集，通过实验表明多模态检索是 MKG-RAG 的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 基准大多忽略多模态知识图谱中检索环节的挑战，多模态知识异构、跨模态对齐困难，现有检索器难以有效处理，因此需要专门的基准来诊断和推动该方向的发展。

Method: 从两个多模态知识图谱（通用领域和医学领域）构建基准，采用基于 LLM 的策展流程：过滤低效用知识、生成结构化的查询及其精确监督信号、系统覆盖多种模态配置，并设计了独立的检索评估和端到端生成评估协议。

Result: 实验表明，有效的多模态检索仍然具有挑战性但对端到端 MKG-RAG 性能至关重要，检索质量强烈决定生成结果，不同检索器系列和模态设置下的性能差异显著。

Conclusion: MKG-RAG-Bench 将检索作为一等评估目标，为诊断当前多模态知识图谱 RAG 系统的局限性并推动其发展提供了原则性基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向多模态知识图谱检索增强生成（MKG-RAG）的跨领域评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal KG retrieval evaluation, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal KG RAG benchmark, retrieval bottleneck analysis, cross-domain evaluation, LLM-based curation pipeline, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over knowledge graphs has emerged as a promising approach for grounding large language models, yet existing benchmarks largely overlook the challenges of retrieval in multimodal knowledge graph RAG (MKG-RAG). In practice, retrieval is a critical bottleneck: multimodal knowledge is heterogeneous, difficult to align across modalities, and often poorly served by retrievers designed for unstructured corpora. To address this gap, we introduce MKG-RAG-Bench, a cross-domain benchmark explicitly designed to evaluate retrieval in MKG-RAG. MKG-RAG-Bench is constructed from two multimodal knowledge graphs spanning general and medical domains, and includes carefully aligned question-answering datasets that support controlled evaluation of both retrieval and downstream generation. The benchmark is built using an LLM-based curation pipeline that filters low-utility knowledge, generates structurally grounded queries with exact supervision, and systematically covers diverse modality configurations. Through extensive experiments across representative retriever families and modality settings, we show that effective multimodal retrieval remains challenging yet crucial for end-to-end MKG-RAG performance, and that retrieval quality strongly determines generation outcomes. By isolating retrieval as a first-class evaluation target, MKG-RAG-Bench provides a principled foundation for diagnosing current limitations and advancing multimodal knowledge graph RAG systems.

</details>


### [85] [WatchAct: A Benchmark for Behavior-Grounded Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26443)
*Baiqi Li, Ce Zhang, Yu Fang, Yue Yang, Shangzhe Li, Mingyu Ding, Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WatchAct 是一个基于人类行为视频的机器人操作评测基准，包含 3000 个长时程实例，覆盖事件解析、程序推理、隐式意图推理和情景推理四个认知域，并设计了分离式评测协议。实验表明当前最优系统（Gemini-3.1-Pro + π₀.₅）在仿真中仅达 16.3% 成功率，在真实机器人上仅 14.0%，远低于人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准仅将指令与单张当前图像配对，无法评估模型对观察到的人类行为（如动作顺序、意图、场景变化）的推理能力，而视频中包含语言难以明确表达的空间布局、物体历史和手势等信息。

Method: 构建 WatchAct 基准：将真实人类操作视频与语言指令配对，对齐到可执行的 LIBERO 仿真场景和任务。包含 3000 个长时程实例，覆盖事件解析、程序推理、隐式意图推理和情景推理四个认知域。提出分离式评测协议，分别评估视频到规划的推理（VLM）、oracle 规划下的策略执行、以及规划-策略联合管线的完整任务完成。

Result: 最佳管线 Gemini-3.1-Pro + π₀.₅ 在仿真中仅达 16.3% 成功率，真实机器人上 14.0%。Gemini-3.1-Pro 规划成功率仅 36.8%（人类 97.1%），π₀.₅ 在 oracle 规划下任务成功率仅 21.5%，域外场景降至 10.6%。

Conclusion: 当前系统在基于人类行为推理的机器人操作任务上远未解决，WatchAct 为评估和推动该方向提供了标准化基准和分离式评测协议。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 WatchAct，一个基于人类行为视频的机器人操作评测基准，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。该基准包含视频理解、意图推理、程序推理等任务，用于评估视觉语言模型和机器人策略，符合该子主题的定义。

Importance reason: Benchmark for robot manipulation grounded in human behavior video, but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: robot manipulation benchmark, human behavior video grounding, long-horizon tasks, low current system performance, disentangled evaluation protocol

Abstract: A robot working alongside people must reason about what they have done, in what order, and with what intent. Video carries the spatial layouts, object histories, and gestures that language leaves underspecified, yet today's manipulation benchmarks pair an instruction with a single current image, offering no way to evaluate reasoning over observed human behavior. We introduce WatchAct, a benchmark for robot manipulation grounded in observed human behavior. Each instance pairs a real-world human-action video and a language instruction with an aligned simulator scene and an executable LIBERO task, enabling scalable and reproducible evaluation. WatchAct comprises 3,000 long-horizon instances across 14 tasks in four capability domains drawn from the cognitive demands of watching another agent: parsing events (Event Grounding), recovering procedural structure (Procedural Reasoning), inferring unstated intent (Implicit Intent Inference), and tracking how the scene was changed (Episodic Reasoning). We further propose a disentangled evaluation protocol that separately measures (i)~video-to-plan reasoning by vision-language models, (ii)~policy execution under oracle plans, and (iii)~full task completion by integrated planner--policy pipelines. In both simulation and on a Franka Research 3 robot, current systems remain far from solving WatchAct. The best pipeline, Gemini-3.1-Pro with $\pi_{0.5}$, reaches only 16.3% Success Rate (SR) in simulation and 14.0% on the real robot. Gemini-3.1-Pro attains just 36.8% Plan SR (vs. 97.1% for humans), while $\pi_{0.5}$ reaches only 21.5% Task SR under oracle plans and drops to 10.6% on out-of-domain scenarios. Dataset and code are available at .

</details>


### [86] [FlameVQA: A Physically-Grounded UAV Wildfire VQA Benchmark with Radiometric Thermal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27128)
*Mobin Habibpour, John Spodnik, Niloufar Alipour Talemi, Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: FlameVQA 是一个基于 FLAME 3 数据集的无人机野火监测多选 VQA 基准，利用配对的 RGB 图像和辐射热 TIFF 进行温度接地推理，包含 6 类 34 个问题，评测了多个 MLLM 并发现其在烟雾遮挡和覆盖估计等场景下存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在野火监测等安全关键领域缺乏专门的评测基准，RGB 图像在烟雾、尺度变化和遮挡场景下难以可靠推理，需要结合热红外辐射数据来提升评估的可靠性。

Method: 基于 FLAME 3 数据集构建 FlameVQA 基准，每张图像包含 34 道多选题，覆盖检测、定位、分布/覆盖估计、跨模态推理和飞行规划等 6 类能力。采用 MLLM 辅助标注结合确定性热规则和跨问题一致性检查，再经人工审核确保标签质量。在多个代表性 MLLM 上进行基线评测。

Result: MLLM 在提供显式跨模态线索时表现较好，但在烟雾遮挡下的存在性检测和覆盖估计任务中表现显著不足，表明当前 MLLM 需要领域特定的适应才能更好地支持野火监测。

Conclusion: FlameVQA 为无人机野火监测提供了首个物理接地（辐射热监督）的 VQA 基准，揭示了现有 MLLM 在灾害监测场景中的局限性，为未来领域自适应研究提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 FlameVQA，一个面向无人机野火监测的多选题视觉问答基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。论文构建了新的 VQA 数据集并评测了多个 MLLM，完全符合该子主题的定义。

Importance reason: A specialized VQA benchmark for wildfire monitoring, but lacks direct connection to the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: UAV wildfire VQA benchmark, radiometric thermal supervision, MLLM evaluation, domain-specific dataset, smoke occlusion failures

Abstract: Wildfire monitoring from UAVs requires reliable reasoning over complex aerial scenes, where smoke, scale variation, and occlusions often limit RGB-only interpretation. We introduce FlameVQA, a multiple-choice visual question answering benchmark for UAV-based wildfire intelligence built on FLAME 3, leveraging paired RGB imagery and radiometric thermal TIFFs for temperature-grounded, safety-critical reasoning. FlameVQA includes 34 multiple-choice questions per image spanning six operational capability groups, covering tasks such as detection, localization, distribution/coverage estimation, cross-modal reasoning, and flight planning. To ensure label reliability, we combine MLLM-assisted annotation with deterministic thermal rules and cross-question consistency checks, followed by human auditing. We also evaluate representative MLLMs on FlameVQA to provide baselines for future work. Results show strong performance when explicit cross-modal cues are available, but notable failures on presence detection under heavy smoke and on coverage estimation. These findings suggest that current MLLMs require domain-specific adaptation to better support disaster and wildfire monitoring. The dataset and benchmark code are open-source at

</details>


### [87] [SocialPersona: Benchmarking Personalized Profiling and Response with Multimodal Social-Media Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26654)
*Qinkai Zhang, Yanyan Zhao, Xin Lu, Yulin Hu, Pengtao Han, Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.IR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SocialPersona 是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型能否从社交媒体时间线中推断用户偏好并用于个性化对话的基准，包含 171 位用户的文本、图像、时间戳和 2597 个人工验证的偏好标签。实验表明模型能识别广泛兴趣领域，但在细粒度、近期兴趣和个性化对话方面表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化语言模型评估主要关注模型能否记住用户明确陈述的偏好，而忽略了从用户自然留下的多模态痕迹中推断偏好的更困难能力。作者希望构建一个基准来评估 MLLMs 是否能够从纵向社交媒体时间线中恢复用户的"显示性偏好"（revealed preferences）并在对话中使用这些偏好。

Method: 收集了 171 位非营销类社交媒体用户的纵向时间线数据，包含文本、图像、时间戳，并标注了 2597 个人工验证的偏好标签，覆盖七个兴趣领域，区分稳定兴趣和近期兴趣。设计了两个任务：（1）从多模态上下文中构建结构化用户画像；（2）生成与推断画像对齐的个性化回复。使用多个闭源和开源 MLLM 进行评测。

Result: 模型能够识别广泛的兴趣领域，但在细粒度兴趣和近期兴趣上性能下降；当需要将推断的用户画像用于个性化对话时，性能进一步下降。文本和图像提供了互补的偏好信号。

Conclusion: 跨模态、长时序的用户建模仍然是一个关键挑战，SocialPersona 可以帮助衡量和推动能够推断并利用显示性偏好的智能助手的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SocialPersona 基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）从社交媒体时间线中推断用户偏好并在对话中个性化响应的能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal user profiling from social media, which is peripheral to your core interests in continuous language models and generative world models. Signals: multimodal benchmark, user profiling, personalized dialogue, social media context, preference inference

Abstract: Personalized language-model assistants are often evaluated through a memory lens: can a model recall preferences users have explicitly stated in dialogue? More comprehensive personalization demands a harder capability -- inferring what users care about from the multimodal traces they naturally leave behind. We introduce SocialPersona, a benchmark for evaluating whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can recover revealed preferences from longitudinal social-media timelines and use them in dialogue. Built from longitudinal timelines of 171 everyday, non-promotional social-media users, SocialPersona contains text, images, timestamps, and 2,597 human-verified preference tags across seven interest domains, separating stable interests from recent interests. It supports two tasks: constructing structured user profiles from multimodal context and generating responses aligned with inferred profiles. Experiments with proprietary and open-weight MLLMs show that models can identify broad interest domains, yet their performance drops on fine-grained and recent interests and degrades further when inferred profiles must be used to personalize dialogue. Together with evidence that text and images provide complementary preference signals, these results indicate that robust cross-modal, long-horizon user modeling remains a key challenge, and that SocialPersona can help measure and advance progress toward assistants that infer and act on revealed preferences.

</details>


### [88] [HarmVideoBench: Benchmarking Harmful Video Understanding in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27187)
*Jiajun Wu, Haoyu Kang, Yining Sun, Jiacheng Hou, Heng Zhang, Danyang Zhang, Zhenjun Zhao, Haochi Zhang, Leixin Sun, Eric Hanchen Jiang, Yushan Li, Ruiyu Li, Mengkai Huang, Yan Gao, Xu Zhang, Guancheng Wan*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HarmVideoBench，一个多层次的有害视频理解诊断基准，包含 1379 个视频和 4137 道多选题，从可观察证据、片段内含义和跨片段推理三个层次评估 LVLM 对有害视频的深层理解能力。同时提出 BCR 方法，通过预测推理边界并动态检索上下文，将宏平均从 61.7% 提升至 84.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有害视频基准存在两个主要局限：1）忽略了有害视频的多层次特征，仅将其视为二分类任务，无法捕捉隐式或深层上下文危害；2）完全缺乏解释性理由，仅判断模型是否正确标记视频，而不解释原因，使评估成为黑箱。

Method: 构建 HarmVideoBench 基准，包含 1379 个视频和 4137 道多选题，从三个层次评估：可观察证据（Observable Evidence）、片段内含义（Clip-Internal Meaning）和跨片段推理（Beyond-Clip Reasoning）。同时提出 BCR（Benchmark-aligned Context Retrieval）方法，预测推理边界并仅在需要时动态检索上下文。

Result: 在 19 个主流模型上的评估显示，现有模型在有害视频理解上表现有限。BCR 方法将基线的宏平均从 61.7% 提升至 84.4%，达到当前最优水平。

Conclusion: HarmVideoBench 为有害视频理解提供了更全面的多层次评估框架，BCR 方法有效提升了模型在该任务上的表现，推动了内容审核领域的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 HarmVideoBench，一个针对有害视频理解的多层次诊断基准，包含 1379 个视频和 4137 道多选题，评估 LVLM 在有害视频理解上的深层能力。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on harmful video understanding benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on generation, world models, or continuous representations. Signals: Harmful video benchmark, LVLM evaluation, multi-level reasoning, BCR method, not generation or world model

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently shown immense potential in automated content moderation, sparking growing interest in developing harmful-video benchmarks. However, we identify two primary limitations in existing works: 1) The multi-layered characteristics of harmful videos are overlooked. Existing benchmarks predominantly formulate evaluation as a binary classification task, failing to capture implicit or deep contextual harms. 2) Explanatory rationales are completely absent. Current frameworks measure exclusively whether a model flags a video correctly rather than explaining why, turning evaluation into a black box where models can succeed through superficial shortcuts. To address these problems, we present HarmVideoBench, a multi-layered diagnostic benchmark comprising 1,379 videos paired with 4,137 multiple-choice questions. HarmVideoBench benchmarks three hierarchical dimensions: Observable Evidence, Clip-Internal Meaning, and Beyond-Clip Reasoning, aiming to evaluate models' deep understanding beyond surface cues with carefully balanced and curated samples. We evaluate 19 leading models on HarmVideoBench to assess their multidimensional understanding of harmful videos. Moreover, we introduce BCR, a benchmark-aligned method that predicts reasoning boundaries and dynamically retrieves context only when needed. Experimental results show that BCR substantially improves the base model's performance in harmful video understanding, raising the macro average from 61.7 percent to a state-of-the-art 84.4 percent.

</details>


### [89] [CORTEX: A Structured Reasoning Benchmark for Trustworthy 3D Chest CT MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27264)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik, Anees Ur Rehman Hashmi, Numan Saeed, Muzammal Naseer, Salman Khan, Christoph Lippert*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (62.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CORTEX是一个面向3D胸部CT MLLM的结构化推理基准，通过四阶段诊断轨迹（任务理解→视觉观察→诊断推理→答案合成）恢复放射科医生的推理过程，包含76,177条验证推理轨迹，并提供阶段级评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D胸部CT问答数据集将专家报告简化为仅答案对，丢失了连接发现与结论的推理过程以及临床病史信息，导致无法训练和验证具备可解释推理能力的3D胸部CT MLLM。

Method: 使用前沿大语言模型生成四阶段诊断推理轨迹（任务理解、视觉观察、诊断推理、答案合成），结合自动评分规则和放射科专家审查进行阶段级过滤验证，构建于CT-RATE数据集之上。

Result: 构建了包含76,177条验证推理轨迹的数据集，覆盖开放式VQA、封闭式VQA和报告生成三类任务，提供了结构化监督和阶段级评估协议。

Conclusion: CORTEX为构建和评估3D胸部CT可信推理模型提供了必要的结构化监督和评估协议，填补了该领域空白。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了CORTEX，一个面向3D胸部CT的MLLM结构化推理基准，包含76K+验证推理轨迹和阶段级评估协议。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，是[CANONICAL]子主题，直接匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on medical 3D CT reasoning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords like medical diagnosis. Signals: Medical domain, 3D chest CT, structured reasoning benchmark, MLLM evaluation, negative keyword match

Abstract: Reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has shown strong promise in medical imaging. However, this reasoning is usually free-form text judged only by its final answer, making it hard to interpret and verify, especially in 3D radiology, where a diagnosis should be traceable to evidence in the scan. Existing chest CT question-answering datasets compound this by reducing expert radiology reports to answer-only pairs, dropping the reasoning that links findings to conclusions and omitting the patient history clinicians rely on. As a result, reasoning-capable 3D chest CT MLLMs remain out of reach, as neither the structured supervision needed to train them nor the protocol needed to verify their reasoning yet exists. We introduce CORTEX (Clinically Organized Reasoning and sTructured EXplanation), a structured reasoning benchmark for 3D chest CT. For each question, CORTEX restores the missing reasoning as a four-stage diagnostic trace mirroring a radiologist's workflow: task understanding, visual observation, diagnostic reasoning, and answer synthesis. We generate these traces using frontier large language models with broad medical and general-domain knowledge, then filter and verify them with a stage-level evaluation protocol combining automated rubric scoring with expert radiologist review. Crucially, both the reasoning structure and evaluation rubrics are designed in close collaboration with clinicians. Built on CT-RATE, a large, publicly available chest CT dataset without reasoning annotations, CORTEX comprises 76,177 validated reasoning traces across open-ended VQA, closed-ended VQA, and report generation, providing both the structured supervision and the stage-level evaluation protocol needed to build and evaluate trustworthy reasoning models for 3D chest CT. Our dataset and evaluation code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [90] [NuclearQAv2: A Structured Benchmark for Evaluating Domain-Science Competence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27047)
*Henry Shaowu Yuchi, Michal Kucer, Benjamin H. Sims, Selma Peterson, Emily Taylor*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: NuclearQAv2 是一个面向核工程领域的大语言模型评测基准，包含约1240个问答对，涵盖布尔、数值和文字三类问题。实验表明模型在事实性问题表现良好，但在定量推理和概念理解上仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核工程领域的问题求解不仅需要事实性知识，还需要定量推理和概念理解。现有评测基准缺乏对这类高度技术性领域的系统性评估，因此需要构建一个专门针对核工程知识的评测基准。

Method: 采用混合流水线构建基准：结合专家撰写的问题、现有数据集以及基于领域技术语料库的 LLM 辅助生成。利用结构化提示实现自动化问题生成和响应评估，构建了约1240个问答对，分为布尔、数值和文字三类。

Result: 在多种 LLM 上评估发现，模型在事实性问题（布尔类）上表现良好，但在定量推理（数值类）和概念理解（文字类）上仍存在显著差距，不同任务类型间性能差异明显。

Conclusion: NuclearQAv2 作为一个可扩展的基准，有效揭示了 LLM 在技术领域中的能力差异，强调了多维度评测框架的重要性，为评估 LLM 在专业领域的能力提供了有力工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 NuclearQAv2，一个用于评估大语言模型在核工程领域知识能力的基准，包含布尔、数值和文字三类问答对。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）[CANONICAL] 子主题，因为论文核心贡献是构建了一个领域科学评测基准。

Importance reason: Domain-specific benchmark for nuclear engineering is not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models. Signals: domain-specific benchmark, nuclear engineering, factual vs. quantitative reasoning, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but ensuring their reliability in highly technical domains remains a significant challenge. In nuclear engineering, problem solving often requires not only factual knowledge but also quantitative reasoning and conceptual understanding. To address the need for systematic evaluation in this domain, we introduce NuclearQAv2, a benchmark for assessing LLMs on nuclear engineering knowledge. The benchmark comprises approximately 1,240 question-answer pairs spanning three categories: boolean, numeric, and verbal. NuclearQAv2 is constructed using a hybrid pipeline that combines expert-authored questions, existing datasets, and LLM-assisted generation from domain-specific technical corpora. By leveraging structured prompting for both automated question generation and response evaluation, the proposed framework enables scalable benchmark construction and evaluation. We evaluate a diverse set of LLMs using NuclearQAv2 and observe substantial performance differences across task types. While the models generally perform well on factual questions, quantitative reasoning and conceptual understanding remain considerably more challenging. These results highlight the importance of multi-faceted evaluation frameworks and establish NuclearQAv2 as a scalable benchmark for assessing LLM capabilities in technical domains.

</details>

### [91] [GeMoE: Gating Entropy is All You Need for Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Routing in MoE-based Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26287)
*Chaoxiang Cai, Minghe Weng, Jie Li, Yibo Jiang, Longrong Yang, Zequn Qin, Xi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (66.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GeMoE 提出了一种基于门控熵的不确定性感知自适应路由方法，将 MoE 中的 token 路由建模为最小描述长度（MDL）问题，通过门控熵动态决定每个 token 激活的专家数量，在保持 99.5% 性能的同时将专家激活稀疏性提升 36.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 MoE 方法采用 Top-k 静态路由策略，无法根据输入变化自适应选择专家数量，导致资源利用次优。作者希望将 token 路由视为信息编码任务，通过 MDL 原则在模型复杂度与性能之间取得平衡。

Method: 将动态路由形式化为最小描述长度（MDL）问题，验证了 MoE 场景下 MDL 与门控熵之间的联系，提出 GeMoE：利用门控熵评估每个 token 的复杂度，自适应地决定每个 token 应激活的专家数量，替代传统的固定 Top-k 路由。

Result: 在多种骨干网络和基准上，GeMoE 相比原始静态路由实现了 99.5% 的平均性能保持率，同时将平均专家激活稀疏性提升了 36.5%。

Conclusion: GeMoE 通过门控熵显式建模模型复杂度与性能的权衡，实现了更高效、自适应的 MoE 路由，验证了 MDL 框架在 MoE 路由设计中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MoE 架构下基于门控熵的自适应路由策略，旨在提升大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的专家激活稀疏性，降低推理成本。这属于多模态模型的高效化与轻量化方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in MoE-based LVLMs, aligning with model efficiency and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: MoE dynamic routing, gating entropy, efficient inference, LVLM, uncertainty-aware

Abstract: With the increase in model parameters and training data, the instruction following and generalization capabilities of Large VisionLanguage Models (LVLMs) have been significantly improved. Based on the Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture, LVLMs expand their parameter capacity while maintaining the inference cost. However, traditional MoE methods employ a Top-k static routing strategy, which fails to account for variations in the input and adaptively select the number of experts, resulting in suboptimal resource utilization. In this paper, we propose viewing token routing as an information encoding task, framing dynamic routing as a Minimum Description Length (MDL) problem in encoding By validating the connection between MDL and gating entropy in the MoE scenario, we introduce Gating Entropy-based Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Routing (GeMoE) for MoE. Unlike traditional static or heuristic-based dynamic routing methods, GeMoE explicitly models the trade-off between model complexity and performance. By using gating entropy to assess the complexity of tokens, GeMoE adaptively determines the number of experts each token should engage. On a wide range of backbones and benchmarks, our method achieves 99.5% average performance retention compared to the original static routing, while improving average expert activation sparsity by 36.5%.

</details>


### [92] [TOPS: First-Principles Visual Token Pruning via Constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets for Efficient MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27161)
*Tinghao Wang, Yichen Guo, Rui Huang, Zheng Lu, Qizhe Zhang, Chenxi Li, Yuan Zhang, Jiajun Cao, Zhirong Shen, Yaosong Du, Guangyan Gan, Wenya Wang, Lin William Cong, Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (89.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，通过信息论分析提出视觉 token 剪枝的三个基本原则（任务相关性、信息覆盖、语义多样性），并据此设计 TOPS——一种无需训练、模型无关的 token 剪枝模块，可在 LLaVA-NeXT 上移除 77.8% 的视觉 token 而保持甚至提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 中大量视觉 token 带来巨大计算开销，现有剪枝方法（基于注意力或多样性）缺乏对剪枝目标的系统性形式化定义，难以在保留关键信息的同时有效压缩。

Method: 通过信息论分析自上而下推导出 token 选择的三原则：任务相关性（Task Relevance）、信息覆盖（Information Coverage）、语义多样性（Semantic Diversity）。基于这些原则设计 TOPS，一种无需训练、模型无关的剪枝模块，可即插即用于多种 MLLM。

Result: 在 7 种 MLLM 骨干和 14 个基准上，TOPS 在多种剪枝设置下均优于现有方法。在 LLaVA-NeXT 上，移除 77.8% 视觉 token 后，7B 和 13B 模型分别保持 100.0% 和 100.6% 的性能，表明剪枝冗余 token 有时还能缓解幻觉。

Conclusion: TOPS 通过第一性原理推导的 token 剪枝原则，实现了高效且有效的视觉 token 压缩，为未来轻量化 MLLM 设计提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视觉 token 剪枝方法 TOPS 以加速 MLLM 推理，属于 MLLM 高效化/轻量化方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient MLLM inference via principled token pruning, aligning with user's high priority on model efficiency and unified multimodal models. Signals: training-free token pruning, first-principles information-theoretic analysis, 77.8% token removal with performance preservation, model-agnostic and applicable to various MLLMs, strong empirical results on LLaVA-NeXT

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, but their efficiency is limited by the large number of visual tokens, which introduces substantial computational overhead. Visual token pruning offers a natural solution, yet existing methods are imperfect: attention-based criteria tend to retain redundant tokens, while diversity-based criteria are often agnostic to user instructions. Even methods that combine multiple criteria still lack a principled formulation of the intrinsic objective of token pruning. In this paper, we revisit visual token pruning from a first-principles perspective and formulate it as constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets. Through a top-down information-theoretic analysis, we identify three fundamental principles for effective token selection: Task Relevance, Information Coverage, and Semantic Diversity. Based on these principles, we propose TOPS, a training-free and model-agnostic pruning module that can be applied to various MLLMs. Extensive experiments on 7 MLLM backbones and 14 benchmarks demonstrate that TOPS outperforms prior methods under diverse pruning settings. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, TOPS removes 77.8% of visual tokens while preserving 100.0% and 100.6% performance on its 7B and 13B models, respectively, suggesting that pruning redundant visual tokens can sometimes mitigate hallucination and inspire future lightweight MLLM design.

</details>

### [93] [Unison: Benchmarking Unified Multimodal Models via Synergistic Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26984)
*Jinyu Liu, Xincheng Shuai, Henghui Ding, Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (87.87) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Unison是一个包含2169个样本的统一多模态模型评测基准，专门评估模型在理解与生成协同任务上的表现，涵盖内部一致性、理解引导生成、生成引导理解和相互增强四个维度，并提供了Unison-Judge评估模型以对齐人类判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型虽然同时具备理解与生成能力，但评测方法仍将两者割裂开来，忽略了理解与生成之间的协同效应。缺乏一个能够系统评估两者联合能力的基准。

Method: 构建了包含2169个高质量统一任务样本的Unison基准，设计四个评测维度：内部一致性、理解引导生成、生成引导理解和相互增强。提供统一和解耦两种评测轨道以进行细粒度归因分析，并训练了Unison-Judge评估模型以对齐人类偏好。

Result: 对多个SOTA统一多模态模型进行了系统评测，揭示了当前模型在理解与生成协同任务上的关键局限性，并指出了有前景的未来研究方向。

Conclusion: Unison填补了统一多模态模型联合评测的空白，通过细粒度诊断揭示了现有模型的不足，为未来统一多模态模型的发展提供了方向性指导。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了Unison基准，用于评测统一多模态模型的联合理解与生成能力，属于多模态生成与理解领域的评测基准工作。该论文同时匹配multimodal_understanding_generation和multimodal_generation两个方向。在subtopic层面，它属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark），该subtopic在multimodal_understanding_generation和multimodal_generation方向下均有存在，且为[CANONICAL]条目，直接复用。

Importance reason: Directly addresses unified multimodal model evaluation, a priority subtopic, with a novel synergy-focused benchmark. Signals: unified multimodal benchmark, understanding-generation synergy, human-aligned evaluation, diagnostic evaluation tracks, covers internal consistency and mutual enhancement

Abstract: Unified multimodal models capable of both understanding and generation have achieved remarkable strides. However, despite their unified designs, existing evaluations typically assess understanding and generation capabilities in isolation, overlooking the synergy between comprehension and generation. To bridge this gap, we introduce Unison, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 2,169 high-quality unified task samples, designed to evaluate joint understanding and generation in unified multimodal models. Unison offers three key strengths: 1) Comprehensive Dimensions: Unison encompasses internal consistency, understanding-guided generation, generation-guided understanding, and mutual enhancement to enable holistic evaluation. 2) Diagnostic Evaluation: it provides both unified and decoupled tracks for understanding and generation, allowing fine-grained attribution of failure modes and quantitative analysis of the gains from unified modeling. 3) Human Alignment: we also introduce Unison-Judge, an evaluation model well aligned with human judgments to ensure reliable assessment. Based on systematic evaluations of state-of-the-art models on Unison, we uncover critical limitations in current unified multimodal systems and highlight promising directions for future research. Codes, Unison and Unison-Judge are publicly available at .

</details>
