### [94] [Ask, Solve, Generate: Self-Evolving Unified Multimodal Understanding and Generation via Self-Consistency Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27376)
*Ritesh Thawkar, Shravan Venkatraman, Omkar Thawakar, Abdelrahman Shaker, Fahad Khan, Hisham Cholakkal, Salman Khan, Rao Muhammad Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自演进训练框架，使统一多模态大模型（LMM）仅使用无标注图像即可同时提升视觉理解与图像生成能力。框架包含三个内部角色：Proposer（生成视觉问题）、Solver（回答并评估）、Generator（合成图像），通过自一致性信号进行训练，无需人工标注或外部奖励模型。引入 Solver Token Entropy (STE) 稳定学习，并设计多尺度内部评估方案用于图像生成。在 BLIP3o、BAGEL、VARGPT-v1.1 三种架构上验证，理解与生成性能均显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态大模型（同时支持视觉理解与图像生成）仍依赖人工标注、偏好标签或外部奖励模型等后训练监督。本文探索是否可以利用无标注图像，让模型自主提升理解与生成能力，从而摆脱对昂贵人工标注的依赖。

Method: 提出自演进训练框架，包含三个内部角色：(1) Proposer 根据输入图像生成视觉问题；(2) Solver 回答问题并评估答案质量，引入 Solver Token Entropy (STE) 基于 token 级预测不确定性提供连续难度信号；(3) Generator 根据问题-答案对合成图像。训练信号完全来自内部自一致性，无需外部监督。图像生成采用多尺度内部评估方案，结合问答忠实度评分与循环一致性描述。该框架可适配扩散（BLIP3o）、整流流（BAGEL）和自回归（VARGPT-v1.1）三种架构。

Result: 在八项理解评测指标上，该方法一致优于对应基座模型。在 BAGEL 上，MMMU 绝对提升 +3.5%，GenEval 图像生成性能从 82% 提升至 85%。

Conclusion: 本文证明统一多模态模型可以通过自演进方式，仅利用无标注图像和内部自一致性信号，同时提升理解与生成能力。Solver Token Entropy 有效稳定了自训练过程，多尺度内部评估方案为生成质量提供了可靠信号。该框架具有架构通用性，为减少统一多模态模型对人工标注的依赖提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出统一多模态模型（理解+生成）的自演进训练框架，属于"统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model）这一现有子主题，该子主题在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 multimodal_generation 两个方向下均有收录。

Importance reason: Directly advances unified multimodal LMMs with a self-evolving training method that boosts both understanding and generation, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: unified multimodal model, self-evolving training, self-consistency rewards, no external supervision, BLIP3o/BAGEL/VARGPT-v1.1

Abstract: Most unified large multimodal models (LMMs) that support both visual understanding and image generation still rely on curated post-training supervision, such as human annotations, preference labels, or external reward models. We ask whether a unified LMM can improve both abilities autonomously using only unlabeled images. We propose a self-evolving training framework with three internal roles: a Proposer that generates visual questions, a Solver that answers and evaluates them, and a Generator that synthesizes images. Training uses only self-derived consistency signals, without human annotations, preference labels, or task-trained external reward/judge models. To stabilize learning, we introduce Solver Token Entropy (STE), a continuous difficulty signal based on token-level prediction uncertainty that remains useful even when sample-level consistency becomes unreliable. For image generation, we design a multi-scale internal evaluation scheme that combines question-answer fidelity scoring with cycle-consistent captioning. This creates a solver-mediated coupling, where better visual understanding enables more reliable generation assessment and stronger internal training signals. The framework preserves the same role decomposition, reward logic, and training schedule across diffusion-based BLIP3o, rectified-flow BAGEL, and autoregressive VARGPT-v1.1 architectures, requiring only each backbone's native prompting and generation interface. Across eight understanding metrics, our method consistently improves over the corresponding base models. On BAGEL, it achieves a $+3.5\%$ absolute gain on MMMU and improves GenEval image generation performance from $82\%$ to $85\%$. Code and models are publicly released.

</details>

### [95] [From Structure to Synergy: A Survey of Vision-Language Perception Paradigm Evolution in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26196)
*Haoxiang Sun, Tao Wang, Li Yuan, Jian Zhao, Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.MM, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (90.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文是第一篇系统综述MLLM中统一视觉-语言感知范式演进的论文，提出了五阶段分类法，从感知视角梳理了MLLM的发展历程、里程碑方法及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM综述往往将视觉和语言分开讨论，缺乏从统一视觉-语言感知视角的系统性梳理。随着O-series和R-series等模型推动感知智能范式转变，亟需一篇从整体视角审视MLLM感知能力演进的综述。

Method: 论文将MLLM感知形式化为一种内在的、统一的视觉-语言能力，类比人类先天感知；提出五阶段分类法（five-stage taxonomy）来追踪MLLM感知的范式演进，并调研每个阶段的代表性方法与里程碑。

Result: 系统梳理了MLLM感知从早期到当前的发展脉络，识别了关键里程碑和范式转变，并总结了开放挑战与有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述为MLLM感知研究提供了基础性理解和可操作的路线图，有助于推动通向通用多模态智能和AGI的进一步创新。

Classification reason: 该论文是一篇关于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中视觉-语言感知范式演进的综述，系统梳理了MLLM感知能力的发展阶段与代表性方法，属于通用视觉语言模型与MLLM架构的范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" (vlm_mllm_general)。

Importance reason: Provides a timely, systematic taxonomy of MLLM perception evolution, directly supporting the user's priority on unified multimodal models. Signals: Systematic survey, MLLM perception paradigm, unified vision-language, five-stage taxonomy, multimodal large language model

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently made remarkable progress in unifying vision-language understanding and reasoning, especially following the introduction of models such as OpenAI's O-series and DeepSeek's R-series, which have driven a paradigm shift toward perception-centric intelligence. However, there remains a lack of systematic surveys that examine perception from a truly unified vision-language perspective -- one that treats vision and language as an inseparable modality. Existing reviews are often fragmented, focusing separately on either vision or language, and thus rarely capture the cross-modal evolution of perception as an integrated capability. To bridge this gap, we present the first systematic survey of unified vision-language perception in MLLMs. Specifically, we (1) formalize MLLM perception as an intrinsic, unified vision-language capability analogous to human innate perception, (2) introduce a five-stage taxonomy tracing the paradigm evolution of MLLM perception and survey representative methods and milestones at each phase, and (3) identify open challenges and outline promising research directions toward truly general, unified multimodal intelligence. We hope our study will provide both a foundational understanding and an actionable roadmap to foster further innovation on the path toward artificial general intelligence (AGI).

</details>


### [96] [TraMP-LLaMA: Generative Interpretability with Decoupled Instruction Tuning for Facial Expression Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26942)
*Shuchao Duan, Alan Whone, Hossein Rahmani, Jun Liu, Majid Mirmehdi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (73.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TraMP-LLaMA 是一个统一多模态框架，结合 RGB 外观和关键点轨迹，采用解耦指令微调策略，同时进行面部表情质量评估（严重程度评分）和结构化文本报告生成，在 PFED5-plus 数据集上优于现有视频-语言基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部表情质量评估（FEQA）方法仅输出严重程度分数，无法提供可解释的面部运动证据，限制了模型在帕金森病评估中的可解释性和可检查性。

Method: 提出 TraMP-LLaMA 框架，融合 RGB 外观和关键点轨迹两种模态线索，采用解耦指令微调策略分别优化严重程度预测和语言生成任务以减少任务干扰，并扩展 PFED5 数据集为 PFED5-plus，加入专家引导的文本运动描述。

Result: 在 PFED5-plus 上，TraMP-LLaMA 在报告生成任务上优于竞争性视频-语言基线，在联合多表情训练下严重程度预测的 Spearman 秩相关系数比所有竞争方法至少提升 4.39%。

Conclusion: TraMP-LLaMA 通过解耦指令微调有效实现了面部表情质量评估中的可解释性生成，同时提升了严重程度预测性能，为临床评估提供了可检查的文本依据。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TraMP-LLaMA，一个统一多模态框架，结合 RGB 外观和关键点轨迹，采用解耦指令微调策略同时进行面部表情严重程度评分和结构化文本报告生成。这属于视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）在多模态指令微调下的应用，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM"。

Importance reason: Focuses on facial expression quality assessment for Parkinson's, which is outside your priority subtopics and negative-keyword domains. Signals: Multimodal framework, decoupled instruction tuning, facial expression quality assessment, video-language baseline, severity prediction

Abstract: Existing facial expression quality assessment (FEQA) methods typically produce only a severity score, without explicitly communicating the observable facial motion evidence that supports the prediction. This limits interpretability and makes it difficult to inspect the basis of model outputs in Parkinson's disease assessment. To address this gap, we propose TraMP-LLaMA, a unified multimodal framework that jointly predicts severity scores and generates structured textual reports from facial motion cues. The framework integrates RGB appearance and landmark trajectory cues, and adopts a decoupled instruction-tuning strategy to reduce task interference between severity prediction and language generation. To support this task, we further extend the PFED5 dataset with expert-guided textual motion descriptions and construct PFED5-plus. Experiments on PFED5-plus show that TraMP-LLaMA outperforms competitive video-language baselines in report generation and achieves the best severity prediction performance among the compared methods under joint multi-expression training, improving Spearman's rank correlation by at least 4.39 percent over all competing methods. The text annotations and code are available at .

</details>


### [97] [Empowering GUI Agents via Autonomous Experience Exploration and Hindsight Experience Utilization for Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27330)
*Tianyi Men, Zhuoran Jin, Pengfei Cao, Yubo Chen, Kang Liu, Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PEEU 方法，通过自主环境探索和事后经验利用，使小规模开源 MLLM（7B）在 GUI 智能体任务规划中超越大模型（Qwen2.5-VL-32B），并提出了 TDHAF 框架分析组合泛化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模开源 MLLM 在 GUI 智能体任务中成本低且保护隐私，但任务规划能力弱、跨网站泛化差，而现有方法依赖大规模人工标注或大模型蒸馏，难以兼顾效率与泛化能力。

Method: 提出 PEEU（Planning Experience Exploration and Utilization）方法，包含两个阶段：1）自主经验探索：智能体在 GUI 环境中自主探索并收集成功/失败经验；2）事后经验利用：利用事后经验合成严格对齐的高层训练数据。同时提出 TDHAF（Task Decomposition Hierarchical Analysis Framework）框架，从低、中、高三个任务粒度系统分析组合泛化行为。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，7B 模型达到 30.6% 准确率，超越 Qwen2.5-VL-32B。分析表明掌握低层原子技能不能保证高层规划能力，而高层任务训练能带来更强的分布外泛化。

Conclusion: 构建高层事后任务并利用经验对于小规模 MLLM 的分布外规划能力至关重要，PEEU 方法有效提升了小模型在 GUI 智能体任务中的规划泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于小规模开源 MLLM 的 GUI 智能体任务规划，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉语言模型与 MLLM 范畴。论文提出的 PEEU 方法通过自主经验探索和事后经验利用来提升小模型的任务规划能力，属于 MLLM 在 GUI 智能体场景中的应用与改进，与 canonical 子主题 vlm_mllm_general 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on GUI agents and task planning, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: GUI agent, task planning, small MLLM, experience exploration, compositional generalization

Abstract: Multimodal web agents can assist humans in operating repetitive GUI tasks, where effective task planning is essential for decomposing complex tasks into executable actions. While small open source MLLMs are cost efficient and privacy preserving compared with commercial large models, they suffer from weak planning and limited cross website generalization. To address these limitations, we introduce the planning experience exploration and utilization (PEEU) method, which autonomously explores environments to discover experiences and utilizes hindsight experience to synthesize strictly aligned, high level training data. To quantitatively analyze the generalization behaviors driving this performance, we propose the task decomposition hierarchical analysis framework (TDHAF) to systematically study compositional generalization across three task granularities: low, middle and high levels. Our analysis reveals that mastering low level atomic skills does not guarantee high level planning competence, while high level task training yields stronger OOD generalization. Experiments on real world benchmarks demonstrate PEEU's superior effectiveness: our 7B model achieves 30.6% accuracy, outperforming the much larger Qwen2.5-VL-32B model. These demonstrate constructing hindsight high level tasks and leveraging experiences is crucial for OOD planning abilities of small MLLMs.

</details>

### [98] [On-board Remote-Sensing Foundation Models for Unsupervised Change Detection of Disaster Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27018)
*S. Ramírez-Gallego*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (40.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于 ResNet (RSFM) + FPN 的无监督变化检测方法，用于卫星遥感影像的灾害事件检测。该方法利用预训练的遥感基础模型作为编码器，结合未训练的 FPN 架构，在潜空间中检测连续轨道过境间的语义偏移，无需标注数据即可实现高效、高分辨率的异常检测，且无需专门训练即可跨地形和传感器泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督变化检测方法需要大量标注数据，且针对特定任务训练的模型难以泛化到不同地形和传感器。卫星在轨资源有限，需要一种轻量、无监督、可自主触发高分辨率捕获的检测方法，以最大化任务效用。

Method: 采用预训练的遥感基础模型（RSFM，基于 ResNet）作为统一编码器提取特征，结合特征金字塔网络（FPN）架构进行多尺度特征融合。通过比较连续轨道过境间潜空间的语义偏移来检测异常，FPN 部分无需训练（training-free），利用其内在先验实现图像级生成和高分辨率映射。

Result: 该方法在多种地形和传感器数据上实现了与有监督方法可比的检测性能，且无需专门训练和大量开发工作，具备良好的泛化能力和定制化灵活性。

Conclusion: 基于 RSFM 的无监督变化检测方法为卫星在轨自主灾害监测提供了一条高效、可泛化的路径，消除了对标注数据和专门训练的依赖，有望提升遥感任务的自主性和资源利用效率。

Classification reason: 论文研究遥感基础模型（RSFM）用于无监督变化检测（灾害事件），属于遥感图像理解与变化检测任务，与多模态理解中的视觉问答/文档理解方向下的遥感/地球观测理解子领域相关。虽然该论文更偏向遥感变化检测，但现有 taxonomy 中最接近的 canonical 子主题是 multimodal_vqa_doc（视觉问答与文档理解），因其涵盖遥感/地球观测理解类任务。

Importance reason: Focuses on remote sensing change detection, which is a negative keyword and outside all priority subtopics. Signals: Remote sensing, Unsupervised change detection, Disaster monitoring, Foundation model, On-board processing

Abstract: Remote Sensing Foundation Models (RSFMs) have emerged as a powerful alternative to supervised models for Earth Observation, allowing satellites to autonomously trigger high-resolution captures or adjust tasking parameters upon detecting an anomaly, thereby maximizing the utility of the mission's limited power and computational resources. RSFMs are versatile, unified encoders that optimize onboard storage for multiple orbital applications while ensuring high-fidelity feature extraction. In particular, unsupervised change detection with RSFMs offers a well-informed and transformative path for disaster monitoring without expensive labels. In this paper, we present a novel unsupervised detection method based on ResNet (RSFM) + FPN which identifies a wide spectrum of anomalies by detecting subtle semantic shifts in the latent space between successive orbital passes. By relying on an untrained FPN architecture and its intrinsic priors, the system achieves efficient image-level generation and higher resolution mapping with minimal effort (training-free) compared to previous proposals (patch-based, trained). And by replacing tailored models with RSFMs, we can achieve comparable results through an approach that eliminates the need for bespoke training and extensive development effort and adds customization, while ensuring high-performance generalization across diverse terrains and sensors.

</details>

### [99] [Forget, Anticipate and Adapt: Test Time Training for Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26515)
*Rajat Modi, Sebastian Noel, Xin Liang, Yogesh Singh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Frame Forgetting Network (FFN)，一种针对长视频的测试时训练方法。FFN仅使用滑动窗口中的三帧（退出帧、当前帧、下一帧）进行TTT更新，并通过定义"惊喜度"指标实现自适应窗口大小调整，大幅降低计算开销。同时构建了EpicTours数据集（最长3小时的城市漫游视频），在密集分割、视频分类和深度估计任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTT方法在长视频上存在两个问题：1) 使用滑动窗口处理过去所有帧，计算量随窗口大小线性增长，数小时视频难以处理；2) 时序相近的帧内容相似时仍进行TTT更新，浪费大量计算。需要一种高效的长视频TTT方法。

Method: 提出Frame Forgetting Network (FFN)：1) 滑动窗口内仅保留三帧——退出帧、当前帧和下一帧，通过帧间关系保留时序上下文；2) 定义"惊喜度"（surprise）指标，量化新帧相对于已见帧的新信息量，据此动态调整有效窗口大小；3) 构建EpicTours数据集，包含长达3小时的城市漫游视频。

Result: 在密集分割、视频分类任务上优于现有TTT方法，且能泛化到深度估计任务；在多小时长视频上保持高效计算，验证了自适应窗口算法的有效性。

Conclusion: FFN通过帧遗忘机制和自适应窗口策略，显著降低了长视频TTT的计算开销，同时保持了时序建模能力，为长视频理解提供了一种实用的测试时训练方案。

Classification reason: 论文关注长视频的测试时训练（TTT）方法，涉及视频理解中的时序建模与自适应窗口机制，属于视频理解与时序推理方向。该论文提出的FFN方法针对长视频（数小时）的密集分割、分类和深度估计任务，与[视频理解与时序推理]这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel test-time training for long videos but outside core priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: test time training, long video understanding, adaptive windowing, EpicTours dataset, dense segmentation

Abstract: Test Time Training (TTT) is a mechanism in which a model adapts to an incoming test-sample by performing some self-supervised (SSL) task and updating its weights even during inference. This procedure does not require labels at test-time. This paper focuses on TTT for long-videos. A major concern with existing approaches is: 1) they perform TTT updates using a sliding window containing frames in the past, whose compute increases linearly with the size of window. This becomes computationally intractable when the videos are hours long. 2) TTT is performed even when temporally close frames look similar, thereby consuming a lot of compute. We present the Frame Forgetting Network (FFN) that: 1) operates on only three frames within the sliding window, namely the frame that exits, the current frame and the frame after that. The model still manages to retain temporal context and work for hours long-videos; 2) mathematically define a surprise metric: how much new information the incoming frame contains with respect to the past seen frame. This facilitates determining how to modify the effective window size during TTT and constitutes the core mechanism of an adaptive windowing algorithm. Additionally, we curate a dataset EpicTours containing up to 3 hour long videos of walking city-tours, whereas earlier datasets on this problem were only 5 min long. We demonstrate FFNs empirical effectiveness on dense-segmentation, video classification tasks, generalization to depth-estimation, and multi-hour long videos.

</details>


### [100] [ProtoKV: Streaming Video Understanding under Delayed Query with Summary-State Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26762)
*Le Tu Ngoc Minh, Jinyeong Lim, Dongsu Han*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (62.78) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ProtoKV 提出一种恒定内存占用的流式视频理解记忆机制，用固定容量的摘要状态（summary state）表示远历史，结合精确近窗口 KV 缓存和语义-空间原型库，在延迟查询场景下显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解中，查询可能异步到达，而关键视觉线索可能在查询到达前已被后续帧冲淡或驱逐。现有方法在有限 GPU 内存下难以同时保留远历史中的关键信息并满足低延迟要求。

Method: ProtoKV 维护一个精确的近窗口 KV 缓存，并将更早的历史内容聚合为固定容量的语义-空间原型库（prototype bank），附带残差统计信息。查询时，每个原型通过有界伪 token 接口暴露，与标准注意力机制兼容。

Result: 在相同内存预算和查询时间成本下，ProtoKV 在长延迟场景的 SVU 基准上相比 token 保留基线方法准确率提升高达 12.5 个百分点，且增益随查询延迟增加而增大。

Conclusion: ProtoKV 通过摘要状态记忆机制有效解决了流式视频理解中的延迟查询挑战，在恒定内存占用下实现了更好的远历史信息保留，为流式视频理解提供了高效且兼容现有注意力机制的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究流式视频理解（streaming video understanding）中延迟查询下的高效记忆机制，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频理解与时序推理" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: ProtoKV's summary-state memory for streaming video understanding is a novel efficiency technique that aligns with the user's priority on efficient inference and unified multimodal models. Signals: efficient inference, streaming video understanding, constant memory, delayed query, summary-state memory

Abstract: Streaming video understanding (SVU) must answer queries that arrive asynchronously while visual tokens stream continuously under strict GPU-memory and query-time latency budgets. A key challenge is delayed query: decisive cues may appear briefly, yet many subsequent updates occur before the query arrives, increasing the risk that those cues are evicted or diluted under bounded memory. We propose ProtoKV, a constant-footprint SVU memory that represents far history as a fixed-capacity summary state rather than retaining token instances. ProtoKV keeps an exact near-window KV cache and aggregates older content into a semantic-spatial prototype bank with residual statistics. At query time, each prototype is exposed through a bounded pseudo-token interface that is drop-in compatible with standard attention. Under matched budgets and comparable query-time cost, ProtoKV improves accuracy by up to 12.5 points over token-retention baselines on SVU benchmarks in the long-delay regime, with gains that grow as query delay increases.

</details>


### [101] [Confidence-Aware Tool Orchestration for Robust Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26904)
*Yangfan He, Yujin Choi, Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (61.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Robust-TO，一个置信感知的工具编排框架，通过显式评估每帧的可信度来增强视频推理模型的鲁棒性。它组织多种视觉感知工具，基于可靠性-相关性分数选择可信帧，并通过三级证据合成与置信-成本 GRPO 奖励联合优化正确性、证据可靠性和效率。在八个任务上，Robust-TO 在干净输入上达到 56.4% 准确率，超越最强开源基线 10.6%，在五种真实扰动下保持 54.3% 准确率，且准确率下降最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推理语言模型隐式假设每帧输入同样可靠，导致在运动模糊、眩光、遮挡等真实扰动下性能大幅下降（15-30%p），且模型对视觉证据退化毫无察觉。作者将此称为"盲目信任问题"（Blind Trust Problem），亟需一种能感知帧级可信度的鲁棒视频理解框架。

Method: Robust-TO 是一个智能体视频理解框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 统一证据接口——将多种视觉感知工具（检测、跟踪、OCR、动作识别等）组织起来，每个工具接收子查询和由可靠性-相关性分数选择的可信帧，返回统一格式的证据（预测结果、时间定位、校准的可靠性分数）；(2) 三级证据合成——根据校准的可靠性分数将证据分为高/中/低三个置信层级进行加权融合；(3) 置信-成本 GRPO 奖励——联合优化答案正确性、证据可靠性和推理效率。

Result: 在涵盖八个任务的视频推理基准上，Robust-TO 在干净输入上达到 56.4% 平均准确率，超越最强开源基线 10.6%，并超过 Gemini-2.5-Pro（46.2%）。在五种真实扰动（运动模糊、眩光、遮挡等）下，Robust-TO 保持 54.3% 平均准确率，比最强开源基线高 5.8%，且在所有对比方法中干净到扰动输入的准确率下降最小。

Conclusion: Robust-TO 通过显式建模帧级可信度，有效解决了视频推理模型在真实扰动下的"盲目信任问题"，显著提升了鲁棒性。该框架在多种扰动下均能保持接近干净输入的性能，为构建更可靠的视频理解系统提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Robust-TO，一个面向视频理解的置信感知工具编排框架，解决视频推理模型在扰动下的鲁棒性问题。核心任务为视频问答与视频推理，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的「视频理解与时序推理」[CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Strong on video understanding robustness but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Confidence-aware tool orchestration, Robust video reasoning under perturbations, GRPO reward for reliability and efficiency, Outperforms Gemini-2.5-Pro, No continuous token or flow matching focus

Abstract: Video reasoning language models implicitly assume that every input frame is equally reliable. This leads to what we term the Blind Trust Problem: under realistic perturbations such as motion blur, glare, or occlusion, frontier video reasoning models can suffer 15-30%p accuracy drops on real-world embodied benchmarks, while remaining unaware that their visual evidence has been degraded. To address this challenge, we propose Robust-TO, an agentic video understanding framework that explicitly integrates per-frame trustworthiness into every stage of reasoning. Robust-TO organizes heterogeneous visual perception tools under a unified evidence interface. Each tool receives a sub-query derived from the original question and a set of trustworthy frames selected by the reliability-relevance score. It returns evidence in a shared format: a concrete prediction (e.g., a bounding box, motion trajectory, recognized text, or action label), temporal grounding, and a calibrated reliability score. During reasoning, these calibrated scores guide evidence weighting in a three-tier synthesis process (high/medium/low) and define a confidence-cost GRPO reward that jointly optimizes correctness, evidence reliability, and efficiency. On two video reasoning benchmarks spanning eight tasks, Robust-TO achieves 56.4% average accuracy on clean inputs, surpassing the strongest open-source baseline by 10.6%p and outperforming Gemini-2.5-Pro (46.2%). Under five realistic corruption types, Robust-TO maintains 54.3% average accuracy, 5.8%p above the strongest open-source baseline, while exhibiting the smallest clean-to-corrupted accuracy drop among all compared methods.

</details>
